# STATUS — Phase 1 Tracker

> Live tracker of what's done, what's in flight, what's blocked, and what's next across all child apps. The coordinator session (this repo) reads it first to answer "what should I work on next?"

## Wave 38 — v3.0.16 Monthly-membership VSC plans (ADR 28, 2026-06-01)

**Trigger:** User asked to support monthly-membership VSC products (M2M / "Residual" / RAP) in the protection workflow. StoneEagle GetRates returns these (OMEGA TPA) with `RateClassMoney.TermMile.Mileage == 999999` and month-by-month terms; they were previously **silently dropped** (Wave-24 filter). They're billed as a recurring monthly charge — no down-payment, no finite months-to-pay, unlimited term + miles.

**Locked decisions:** (1) 999999 sentinel = authoritative detector (plan-code allowlist + "Residual" = telemetry only); (2) Step-5 global term↔monthly switch; (3) config split — pricing in `org.protection_billing.monthly_membership`, presentation in `plan_catalog`/`plan_overrides`.

**Phase A — coordinator canon (LANDED):**
- `canon/plan-mappings.json` — new `monthly_membership` block (sentinel + secondary signals); plan_catalog entries **40=good, 41=good, 42=better, R6=good** (R6 changed from better; DocuSeal IDs for 40/41/42 = null/TBD). `vehicle_class_rule` comment clarified inclusive bounds.
- `canon/org-registry.json` — new `protection_billing.monthly_membership` per org `{ enabled, default:{term_to_use:1, markup_dollars:25, florida_markup_dollars:22}, by_plan_code:{}, discount:{max_percent:15, max_dollars:10, disabled_in_states:[FL]} }`. `term_to_use:1` because SE `<Cost>` is **total-at-term** (cumulative) — term-1 = true per-month cost.
- `canon/_version` → `2026-06-01-v3016-monthly-membership-vsc`. Synced to all 5 child apps.

**Phase B — coordinator packages (LANDED):**
- `packages/integrations/product_admin/stoneeagle.js` — replaced the 999999 **drop** with **grouping**: sentinel rows group by `planCode::deductible` into one `billing_model:'monthly_subscription'` product carrying a `monthly_terms[]` array, `monthly_charge`, `mileage:null`, `unlimited_mileage:true`, `monthly_signals`. Term products gained `billing_model:'term_total'`. `mergeNewUsed` dedupes monthly by id (rate-class-agnostic). Opt-in synthetic demo fixture via localStorage `blinker.dev.monthly_membership_demo`.
- `packages/utils/protection-pricing.js` — `resolveMonthlyMembershipPricing()` (cost-at-`term_to_use` + flat markup, FL split, nearest-term fallback) + `getOrgMonthlyMembershipSnapshot()`. `computePlanPrice` untouched; monthly bypasses `max_plan_price`.
- `packages/utils/protection-addons.js` — add-on passthroughs suppressed for monthly (`triggeredPassthroughKeys` early-returns `[]`) — monthly plans carry no options; conditional passthroughs were leaking term add-on costs onto monthly cards.
- `packages/utils/vehicle-class.js` — New/Used gate operators `<`→`<=` (inclusive: 3 model years or less AND 36,000 mi or less). Fixed a 2024 Bronco @ 36,000mi classifying 'used' (only the U GetRates call fired); now 'new' → fires N+U.
- `architecture/28-monthly-membership-vsc.md` — new ADR (D1–D7 + resolved risks); registered in CLAUDE.md index.

**Phase C — protection-portal (dispatched, LANDED):** `plan-selector.js` forks `{plans, monthly, hasMonthly, debug}` (missing `billing_model`⇒term_total; monthly tier via catalog→tier_hint→name, fixing always-good; bypasses term/miles optimizer + max_plan_price); `listMatchingPlans(mode:'term'|'monthly')`. RecommendedCoverage global term↔monthly switch (clears stale down_payment/months on flip). Customize monthly filter + monthly-derived deductible options. Confirm `isMonthly` branch (dueToday=monthly_charge, hides down-payment/months, monthly discount caps). PlanCard monthly display (Term/Miles="Unlimited", "$X/mo · cancel anytime", monthly_charge). ProtectionDevControls demo toggle. New `plan-selector.test.js` — **11/11 pass** (incl. catalog-wins lock-in test).

**Phase D — mission-control (dispatched, LANDED):** super `OrgConfigSections/Protection.jsx` editable monthly_membership config (enabled + default + per-plan-code table + discount caps + state chips); admin `OrgConfiguration.jsx` read-only mirror; `PlanCatalog.jsx` "Monthly" badge from the allowlist.

**Real-data smoke corrections (2026-06-01, OMGA UAT proxy):** term_to_use 12→1 (was pricing off the 12-mo cumulative cost: R6 showed $385, now $55); tiers 41/R6=good + 42=better; New/Used inclusive fix; no add-ons on monthly; Term/Miles="Unlimited"; deductible filter derives from monthly products. Live result: GOOD=R6 $55/mo, BETTER=42 $74/mo, no Best.

**Open loose ends:**
- **No real 999999 fixture** — synthetic demo only; capture a real OMGA monthly GetRates and add a regression test in the exact shape.
- **DocuSeal template IDs** for 40/41/42 unset (admin Plans tab; R6='3').
- **Seed pricing** ($25 markup, 15%/$10 discount) needs product confirmation (`term_to_use:1` is correct).
- Confirm R6's true `<DeductAmt>` in the API-responses modal ($0 expected per prior UAT).

**LANDED + PUSHED 2026-06-01** — `blinker-platform` (canon + packages + ADR 28 + CLAUDE.md), `protection-portal` (Phase C + synced canon), `mission-control` (Phase D + synced canon), synced-canon-only commits in `insurance-portal` / `customer-portal` / `refinance-prototype`.

## Wave 37 — v3.0.15 PDF (Contact-details gate + timeline actor attribution, 2026-05-16)

**Trigger:** User handed coordinator `/Users/calbertson/Downloads/Blinker Platform - v.3.0.15.pdf` (2 tasks). Task 1 — the insurance opportunity can't generate an embedded-insurance link until the contact has email + phone + a full address; reuse the mc "Add contact" dedup/household + ZIP-decode + Google-Places logic, in shared components. Task 2 — each CoPilot left-rail timeline (insurance/protection/refi) must show who did each step (Agent/Consumer/System); when the agent sends a consumer link, the handed-off step goes read-only for the agent.

**Phase A — coordinator-direct (LANDED in this session):**
- `architecture/27-contact-details-gate-and-timeline-actors.md` — new ADR, 9 decisions (D1–D9), cross-refs 06/23/24/25/26.
- `canon/ghl-status.json` — added `actor` (`agent`|`consumer`|`system`) to all 16 insurance statuses + `_actor_principle` doc string. `actor` is distinct from `signal_source` (which system reported it) and `role` (persona visibility). Static per-status mapping (EI flow is deterministic): agent originates+sends; consumer opens/captures/views/binds on EI's microsite; system handles cascade+errors.
- `canon/_version` → `2026-05-16-v3015-contact-gate-and-timeline-actors`. Synced into all child apps.
- `packages/utils/contact-identity.js` — NEW. `findContactMatch` + `buildHouseholdRelationship` + `HOUSEHOLD_RELATIONSHIP_KINDS` graduated from mc `contact-form.js` (ADR 27 D1). Pure, workflow-agnostic. `validateContactForm` stayed mc-local (form-shape-bound).
- `packages/components/ContactDedupeCard.jsx` — NEW. The same-name + different-name match cards graduated from mc `AddContactModal` (ADR 27 D2). Renders a `findContactMatch` result; `disabled` prop for read-only states.

**Phase B — two dispatches (disjoint repos → parallel):**
- **37a — insurance-portal (LANDED `5007e82`):** Task 1 + the insurance half of Task 2. `LeadOriginationForm` gains an `address` slice + the shared `AddressBlock` (prefilled from `contact.addresses`); `formIsValid`/`validate()` now require a complete address; per-org dedup via `findContactMatch` against `blinkerApi.contacts.list()` surfaced through `ContactDedupeCard` + a DEV CONTROLS dedupe-match emulation block; once `workflow.consumer_link.url` exists the whole form goes read-only ("contact details are locked"). `npm run build` clean.
- **37b — mission-control (LANDED `1b7c37d`):** Task 2 timelines. New `src/lib/timeline-actor.jsx` (`resolveActorLabel` + `<ActorBadge>`) consumed by all 3 timelines; insurance reads actor from canon `actor`, protection/refi from the `step_change` activity `source` (folds `system`/missing → `agent` for a completed step, Phase-1 default). `CoPilotPane.jsx` protection+refi write-throughs now stamp `source: 'agent'` (insurance `status_change` write-through untouched). `contact-form.js` re-points the 3 dedup helpers to `blinker-platform/utils` + re-exports (AddContactModal import surface unchanged). `npm run build` clean.

**Sequencing:** 37a + 37b touch disjoint repos → ran in parallel. Both import from Phase-A packages/canon, so both ran after Phase A.

**Phase 1 limitation (ADR 27 §Phase-1):** the PDF's "send a link at various steps" per-step consumer handoff is delivered for **insurance only** (D9 — the `consumer_link` signal already exists). Protection/refi per-step handoff + read-only views depend on the not-yet-built consumer portal + the Phase-2 customer-link back-channel; deferred. The actor-badge work (D7/D8) lands for all three workflows now and is forward-compatible — a future `source: 'consumer'` renders a Consumer badge with no component change.

**Smoke-test punch list (user, in real browsers):**
- insurance-portal `LeadOriginationForm` — address block prefills/validates; dedup card surfaces under each DEV CONTROLS mode; form locks read-only once a link is minted.
- mc CoPilot — the 3 left-rail timelines render Agent/Consumer/System pills (insurance per-status from canon; protection/refi Agent on completed steps).

**LANDED + PUSHED 2026-05-16** — all 6 repos: `blinker-platform` (ADR 27 + canon + packages + docs), `insurance-portal` (37a + synced canon), `mission-control` (37b + synced canon), and synced-canon-only commits in `protection-portal` / `customer-portal` / `refi-portal`.

**Open loose ends:**
- ADR 27 D2 follow-up: refactor mc `AddContactModal` to consume the shared `ContactDedupeCard` (it kept its local copies this wave — no forced churn).
- insurance-portal 37a: the EI `applicant.address` payload field shape is a placeholder pending the adapter rewrite; `buildHouseholdRelationship` has no Phase-1 persistence target (console.log + telemetry only).

## Wave 36 — v3.0.14 PDF (Insurance quote-flow refinements, 2026-05-15)

**Trigger:** User handed coordinator `/Users/calbertson/Downloads/Blinker Platform - v.3.0.14.pdf` (3 tasks) + the `auto_insurance_company_customer_dropdown.xlsx` carrier list. Make the insurance **quote-only** flow path first-class — strip capture-flow assumptions, default Insurance Quote opps to Quote Only, add a self-reported current-insurance step that drives savings math.

**Phase A — coordinator-direct (LANDED in this session):**
- `architecture/26-insurance-quote-flow.md` — new ADR (5 decisions, cross-refs 06/21/23):
  - **D1 (T1)**: `InsuranceSavingsCard` becomes flow-path-aware — `sent`-phase copy drops "Capture" language for `quote_only`; Find Coverage CTA enabled in `sent` phase for `quote_only` (agent spawns protection while quote pends).
  - **D2 (T2)**: mc timeline header "Capture+quote progress" → flow-path-conditional ("Quote progress" for quote_only); `LeadOriginationForm` FLOW PATH default seeds from `workflow/opportunity.flowPath`.
  - **D3 (T3)**: new agent-side "Current insurance" gate (quote_only) after the mileage gate, before LeadOriginationForm — carrier autocomplete + premium slider + monthly/6mo/12mo cadence toggle; writes `{ currentCarrier, currentCarrierId, currentPremiumCents, premiumCadence }` to workflow root.
  - **D4**: `mapInsuranceWorkflowToSavings` consumes the self-reported premium (normalize to 6mo, savings = current − quoted) — gives quote-only a real savings figure; falls back to EI `savingsAmountCents` when absent.
  - **D5**: new `canon/insurance-carriers.json`.
- `canon/insurance-carriers.json` — NEW. 92 carriers from the user's xlsx "Customer Dropdown" sheet + `other`/`not_sure` catch-alls; `{ id, displayName, parentGroup, website, category }`. Plus `average_premium` (150000c/6mo) + `premium_slider` bounds.
- `canon/_version` → `2026-05-15-v3014-insurance-quote-flow`. Synced (sync script globs `canon/*.json` — new file auto-propagates).
- `STATUS.md` + `PROMPTS.md` § 36.

**Phase B — two dispatches (disjoint repos → parallel):**
- **36a — mission-control:** D1 (`InsuranceSavingsCard` flow-path copy + CTA) + D2a (`RelatedInsuranceProgress` header) + D4 (`insurance-savings-adapter` self-reported-premium math). All mc, single commit.
- **36b — insurance-portal:** D2b (`LeadOriginationForm` flow-path default + opp `flowPath` seeding) + D3 (new `CurrentInsuranceGate` + canon-driven carrier autocomplete + workflow fields). insurance-portal, single commit.

**Sequencing:** 36a + 36b touch disjoint repos. 36a's savings adapter reads workflow fields 36b produces — forward-compatible (null → EI fallback). Both run after Phase A.

**LANDED 2026-05-15** — Phase B: mission-control `183220a` (36a) + insurance-portal `35584df` (36b).

**Wave 36 follow-ups (coordinator-direct):**
- **fu1 (`293a6df`)** — `canon/insurance-carriers.json` dedup: the xlsx Customer Dropdown sheet already ends with 4 "Form option" rows (Other / Not sure / Self-insured / No insurance); the first pass wrongly appended its own → duplicate `not_sure`. Regenerated verbatim from the xlsx: 92 entries (88 carriers + 4 Form-option, fixed slugs `other`/`not_sure`/`self_insured`/`no_insurance`), all unique. Re-synced.
- **fu2 (`ffa5293`)** — seeded explicit `flowPath` on all 13 fixture insurance opps (`quote_only` for opp_ins_001/107/109 at quote_link.* statuses; `capture_and_quote` for the rest). `buildInitial` already reads `opportunity.flowPath` — without the fixture field the quote-only default never triggered. The 4 new workflow fields (`currentCarrier`/`currentCarrierId`/`currentPremiumCents`/`premiumCadence`) are NOT null-seeded in `buildInitial` — 36a confirmed `undefined` behaves identically (savings adapter falls back to the EI path); cosmetic-only, deferred.
- **fu3 (canon `6cd7622` / insurance-portal `05f22e2`)** — per-cadence premium-slider bounds: `premium_slider.by_cadence` (monthly $50–$1,500; 6mo $300–$5,000; 12mo $600–$5,000). `CurrentInsuranceGate` reads `by_cadence[cadence]`, re-bounds + clamps the value on cadence toggle. canon `_version` → `2026-05-15-v3014-insurance-quote-flow-fu1`.
- **fu4 (LANDED — blinker-platform `f4a3bd1`, mission-control `8a8c796`, insurance-portal `61813c1`)** — quote-only estimated savings from the self-reported premium. Smoke testing showed: (1) the timeline `QuoteDetail` hover popover hardcoded "No savings comparison (quote-only path)" — never computed from self-report; (2) force-status skips the `CurrentInsuranceGate`, so force-statused/fixture quote-only opps carried no self-reported data → SavingsCard showed "No carrier comparison". Fixes: ADR 26 D4-fu4 addendum; seeded `current_insurance` block on the 3 fixture quote-only opps (opp_ins_001 State Farm $1,500/6mo, opp_ins_107 Allstate $280/mo, opp_ins_109 Progressive $3,200/yr — all above the $1,200/6mo EI quote → positive savings); mc `buildInitial` seeds the 4 self-reported workflow-root fields from `opportunity.current_insurance`; `QuoteDetail` popover (both `RelatedInsuranceProgress` + insurance-portal `LeadStatusTimeline`, replicas) now computes + shows "Est. savings $X / 6mo vs {carrier}" / "No better rate than {carrier} today" / "No savings comparison". Savings adapter unchanged (already correct). NOTE: the `RelatedInsuranceProgress` header IS flow-path-conditional in code (`183220a`) — a screenshot showing "CAPTURE+QUOTE PROGRESS" for quote rows is a stale dev build; restart the mc dev server.
- **fu5 (LANDED — mission-control `a5a3d12`)** — closes the fu4 related-opp gap. The insurance status write-through (`CoPilotPane.jsx`) snapshots `current_insurance` onto the opp record alongside `summary`; `RelatedInsuranceProgress` `QuoteDetail` resolves `workflowSnapshot ?? opportunity.summary ?? opportunity.current_insurance`.
- **fu6 (LANDED — mission-control `fcd88da`)** — smoke test showed savings STILL absent for a brand-new force-statused quote-only opp. Root cause: the `CurrentInsuranceGate` is a pre-send step gated on `flowPath === 'quote_only'`, but flow path is chosen in the `LeadOriginationForm` which runs AFTER the gate — so the gate is unreachable for a new opp, and force-status skips all pre-send gates anyway. Fix (ADR 26 D6): `InsuranceSavingsCard` is now the always-reachable surface — when a quote-only post-send opp has no `currentPremiumCents`, the card renders a compact inline editor (carrier autocomplete + per-cadence premium slider + cadence toggle); submit writes the workflow + persists `current_insurance` to the opp record; savings recompute live; "Edit current insurance" affordance when data present. Gate stays as the pre-fill path.
- **fu8 HOTFIX (LANDED — mission-control `97fa379`)** — clicking Find Coverage produced a blank page. Root cause: W36-fu3 (`745d2d6`) added `orgId={orgId}` to the `RelatedOppRow` callsite inside `OpportunityContextPane`, but `orgId` is only bound in the separate `CoPilotPane` component — `ReferenceError: orgId is not defined` crashed the render for ANY contact with 2+ opportunities (Find Coverage just creates the second opp). Fixed: `orgId={contact?.org_id ?? null}`, matching the three sibling active-opp timeline callsites. **Lesson:** the W36-fu3 dispatch agent claimed `orgId` was "in scope" — it was not; a one-line cross-component scope error slipped through because the brief trusted the agent's scope read. Verify identifier scope on prop-threading fixes.
- **fu7 (LANDED — mission-control `489cfae`)** — smoke test: the left-rail `QuoteDetail` popover showed est. savings ($680/6mo vs Allstate) but the `InsuranceSavingsCard` still said "No carrier comparison available". Root cause: the card classified its `quoted_with/no_savings` phase purely from `quote.savingsAmountCents` (EI field, null for quote-only) and never called `mapInsuranceWorkflowToSavings` — so 36a's D4 self-reported math (which lives in the adapter) never reached the card; card + popover diverged. Fix (ADR 26 D6-fu7): the card computes `mapInsuranceWorkflowToSavings(workflow)` once and drives the quote.* phase split + savings hero + carrier lines from it. Capture+quote behavior-identical.
- **Open loose end** — the synced canon copies (`insurance-carriers.json` + bumped `_version`) are committed in mission-control (`fcd88da`) but remain UNcommitted in protection-portal / insurance-portal / customer-portal / refi-portal `src/constants/canon/`. Per platform convention each child repo commits its own canon copy — needs a `git add src/constants/canon && git commit` in those four repos when they're next committed/pushed.
- Brand-new insurance opps created from scratch have no `flowPath` → default `capture_and_quote`; the agent picks Quote Only in the FlowPathPicker. Auto-defaulting to Quote Only only applies to opps that carry `flowPath` (fixtures, or a future creation picker).

## Wave 33 — v3.0.13 PDF (Insurance CoPilot post-send layout swap, 2026-05-15)

**Trigger:** User handed coordinator `/Users/calbertson/Downloads/Blinker Platform - v.3.0.13.pdf` (single task). Swap the mc insurance CoPilot post-send layout: move the verbose LeadStatusTimeline from the right pane to a compact left-rail timeline (under VEHICLE / RELATED OPPORTUNITIES); add date separators (Month Day, Year) above first event + when the day changes; per-event hover popovers carrying the per-stage detail blocks (carrier / policy / premium / savings / ID-card / verified pill / etc.). Right pane refocuses on InsuranceSavingsCard ("Insurance at a glance") + ConsumerLink + Notes — agent's primary focus while waiting for consumer EI motion, with Find Coverage CTA (Wave 31b D3) one click away.

**Phase A — coordinator-direct (LANDED in this session):**

- `architecture/23-insurance-copilot-post-send-layout.md` — new ADR (5 decisions, cross-refs 06/08/21):
  - **D1**: Right-pane focus shifts — when active opp is insurance + post-send, suppress LeadStatusTimeline; surface InsuranceSavingsCard. Pre-send unaffected.
  - **D2**: Left-rail compact timeline mounts for active-insurance opp (today only mounts under related-insurance opps). Generalize `RelatedInsuranceProgress` (rename optional). Omit "Open insurance CoPilot →" affordance when rendering for the active opp itself.
  - **D3**: Date separators in long-form `Month Day, Year`. Above first event always; between adjacent events when local-day differs (use `timezoneForOrg(orgId)` already used by insurance-portal LeadStatusTimeline).
  - **D4**: Per-event hover popover using canonical Tooltip pattern (PlanCard MonthlyTooltip — trigger ref + getBoundingClientRect + position:fixed + opacity-transition). Replicate (NOT lift) the 4 detail blocks (CaptureDetail / QuoteDetail / QuoteViewedDetail / PolicyDetail) from insurance-portal LeadStatusTimeline — 2 consumers, 3-strikes rule. Workflow snapshot threaded through as a new prop alongside the existing `opportunity` prop. Native `title=` forbidden.
  - **D5**: insurance-portal AgentView gains `hideLeadStatusTimeline={false}` prop. mc embed passes `true` when D1 conditions hold. Standalone unchanged. Per `feedback_parallel_dev_panels.md`.

**LANDED 2026-05-15** — Phase B: insurance-portal `2217d1b` (33a) + mission-control `5ce5e8e` (33b).

**Wave 33-fu (same-session smoke fixes, 2026-05-15):**
1. **mc posture bug** — `CoPilotPane.jsx` passed `safeWorkflow.status` (a machine_id, e.g. `capture_link.sent`) to `stepFromStatus`, which only matches human labels (`"Capture Link Sent"`). Lookup always missed → `pre_send` fallback → `hideLeadStatusTimeline` never fired → verbose right-pane timeline never suppressed. Fixed: resolve `workflow.status` via `stepFromMachineId`, `opportunity.status` via `stepFromStatus`. mission-control `bc4c9ed`. **Generalizable:** insurance is the only workflow with both human-label + machine_id status forms — always match the resolver to the field's format (`status-step-map.js` docstring spells this out).
2. **insurance-portal post-send layout** — 33a collapsed the `hideLeadStatusTimeline` branch to a single column; v3.0.13 PDF mockup p.2 wants ConsumerLink + Agent Notes side-by-side. Fixed: 2-col grid (`grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_360px]`), ConsumerLinkPanel left / NotesPanel+TagPicker right, ForceStatusBar full-width above. insurance-portal `3fab62a`.

**Wave 33-fu2 (SavingsCard placement, 2026-05-15):** mockup p.2 wants "Insurance at a glance" *inside* the left column above CONSUMER LINK, not as a full-width top band. Since the ForceStatusBar + 2-col grid are owned by insurance-portal AgentView but `InsuranceSavingsCard` is an mc component, added a `savingsCardSlot` render-prop to AgentView (insurance-portal `d87add1`); mc stops rendering the card as a top band and passes it via the slot (mission-control `7ebf64b`). ADR 23 D1+D5 updated with the exact composition + slot mechanism. **Pattern:** when an embedded fragment must place a host-owned component inside a layout the fragment controls, use a render-prop slot — don't relocate the layout ownership.

**Wave 33-fu3 (related-opp popover detail persistence, 2026-05-15):** left-rail timeline popovers for Capture Completed / Quoted / Quote Viewed / Policy Written rendered empty on *related* insurance opps — the rich detail (carrier/premium/policy) lived only on the active-opp live `insuranceWorkflow` and evaporated on opp-switch (mc keeps exactly one live workflow). Fixed (mission-control `281e7f9`, single repo): the Wave 31b-fu4 status write-through now also snapshots `insuranceWorkflow.{capture,quote,policy}` onto the opp record as an additive `summary` block; `buildInitial` restores those on re-seed; `RelatedInsuranceProgress` popover detail resolves `workflowSnapshot ?? opportunity.summary`. ADR 23 D4 updated with the two-source detail model. **Open follow-up (W33-fu4 candidate):** fixture insurance opps in `packages/api/_fixtures/opportunities.json` (opp_ins_002/102/003/104/004/101/103) carry no `summary` → their related-opp popovers stay label+timestamp-only until a fixture-seeding pass.

**Wave 29 follow-ups + flags (not blockers):**

- **29a double-emit of `assignment.assigned` telemetry** — AgentPicker emits it on pick (intent) AND the consumer (Assignment.jsx) emits it again on persist (action, with cascade fields). Both events carry `source: 'assignment_screen' | 'manager_inbox' | 'copilot' | 'bulk_bar'`.
- **29b tag-merge is a read-time illusion in Phase 1** — `agents.json` / `contacts.json` are checked-in canon and can't be mutated; merge writes only to `blinker.tags.v1.merges` overlay, and `list()` consults a merge index to redirect source→dest by both id and name. Visually correct; underlying JSON unchanged. Phase 2 needs real backend write + audit record.

## Wave 28 — Manager MVP (Phase 1B, 2026-05-12)

**Trigger:** User asked the coordinator to design out a Manager view that leads agents in one or more orgs, leaning on single-org-at-a-time. Existing manager view in mission-control was a 3-card "Not wired yet" stub.

**Design landed in coordinator (this commit):**
- `architecture/19-manager-experience.md` — full ADR: IA, 6 screens, multi-org scoping, cross-org assignment policy, suggestion algorithm, reuse map, phasing.
- `packages/api/_fixtures/agents.json` — seeded with 8 agents (Apex 102 + Kings DR 103 + Rewardsco 106) + 2 managers (Taylor Brooks single-org, Morgan Diaz parent-partner multi-org).
- `PROMPTS.md` § 28a–28e — dispatch templates for the 5 mission-control waves.

**Design decisions answered by user:**
- Suggestion algorithm = `0.6 × workload + 0.4 × tag-match` (load-balanced + tag-match, manual override always).
- Coaching notes = manager-only visibility.
- Cross-org assignment = allowed when manager's accessible-orgs includes both source + destination orgs. Parent-partner managers can move opps/contacts/data between child orgs in their tree.
- Resolves canon `personas.json::_TODO[0]` (child-org analytics question) — via opt-in "All my orgs" rollup mode.

**Wave 28 phasing:**
- **28a — Foundation (sequential, must land first):** OrgSwitcher shell control + `NAV_BY_PERSONA.manager` fleshed out + 5 scaffold screens + `packages/api/agents.js` SDK with workload derivation.
- **28b — Team page (parallel after 28a):** AgentRosterTable + AgentProfile right pane + coaching notes.
- **28c — Manager Home (parallel after 28a):** Team KPIs + by-agent strip + funnel + cross-contact activity feed.
- **28d — Manager Inbox (parallel after 28a):** group-by-agent + bulk reassign + Owner=Unassigned + Stuck/API-failure filters + manager-overlay CoPilot affordances.

## (older entries follow) — **Wave 23 ALL PHASES + 2 follow-ups LANDED locally** across 3 repos (8 commits). Phase A canon + ADRs 14/15/16; Phase B1 packages stoneeagle (State, N/U fan-out, term_basis, classifySeError); Phase B2 mission-control nav + AgentReports embed; Phase C protection-portal wizard wiring; **fu1** super_admin SE-error inline diagnostics; **fu2** AssetType resolution + YMMT data refresh from SEFI 17APR2026 reference (164 makes / 3,100 models, drops hardcoded `T`). User smoke surfaced TWO follow-up findings now in backlog: (a) **term_semantics RegulatedRuleId-keyed defaults are wrong** — `Omega EXCL Total Miles` plan rendered as `+72 mo / +100,000 mi` but is actually absolute-from-purchase on both axes; backlog in `architecture/14-term-semantics.md` calls for authoritative mapping derived from real SE GetRates fields + per-plan-code contract review (no name-pattern guessing); (b) **consumer-actionable error CTAs** — backlog in `architecture/15-se-error-handling.md` for VIN-decode and state-not-supported errors. Prior Wave 22: BOTH PHASES + 5 follow-up fixes landed locally (5th = org-config markup math + max_plan_price cost cap; ready to push). 5 user smoke rounds drove the W22 follow-ups. Repo state pre-W23: `blinker-platform` 3 commits ahead of origin/main; `protection-portal` 4 commits ahead; `mission-control` 2 commits + uncommitted canon-sync drift (will commit before push). **Wave 22-fu1 (`blinker-platform d5fa4d2`)** — `fix(stoneeagle): populate product.add_ons_included from rate-class options`. User smoke: T2 "Maintenance Feature included" badge wasn't firing on real OMGA UAT data. Root cause: `normalizeToFixtureShape` was initializing every product with `add_ons_included: []` and never populating it from the rate-class options (only the fixture had the IDs pre-set). Fix: walk each rate-class's `<Option>` elements before pushing the product, push option NAMES into `includedNames[]` when `RetailRate=0` (complimentary / "in the price of the coverage"), then assign `add_ons_included: includedNames`. Non-zero options remain in the global `add_ons[]` catalog only (selectable surcharges). Helper's existing `anyIncludedMatches` substring-matches the option desc against canon `["Extended Maintenance - Annual","Extended Maintenance - monthly"]` directly. **Wave 22-fu1 (`protection-portal a17fab2`)** — `fix(planCard): tooltip escapes overflow-hidden ancestor` (FIRST attempt; insufficient). User smoke: tooltip not visible on `~$XX/mo` line — clipped beneath next sibling card. Initial fix restructured wrapper from `<button>` to `<div role="button">` with `overflow-hidden` moved to inner div. **DID NOT FIX** — the MonthlyTooltip still rendered DEEP INSIDE the inner overflow-hidden div (in the right-side total cell). User reported tooltip still invisible. **Wave 22-fu2 (`protection-portal ce3b765`)** — `fix(planCard): tooltip uses fixed positioning to escape clip ancestors`. Real fix: `MonthlyTooltip` now reads trigger's `getBoundingClientRect()` on `show()`, sets `coords: { top: r.bottom+4, left: r.right }`, renders with `style={{position:'fixed', top, left, transform:'translateX(-100%)'}}` + `z-[60]`. `position: fixed` lifts the tooltip to viewport-root for layout, escaping every overflow-hidden ancestor regardless of nesting depth. Trade-off: coords go stale on scroll/resize while open — acceptable since tooltip auto-hides on mouseLeave/blur. Removed brittle arrow detail. PostHog `track()` + `toggle()` stop-propagation preserved. **Wave 22-fu3 (`mission-control 8a3f981`)** — `fix(vite): proxy /se-rating to SCS UAT for embedded AgentView`. User smoke: standalone protection-portal Real (UAT) toggle returned plans for VIN; SAME vehicle in mc CoPilot embed got "No coverage options came back". Root cause: Wave 21-fu added `/se-rating` proxy to `protection-portal/vite.config.js` ONLY. When AgentView is embedded in mc's tab (mc dev server :5177), the SOAP `fetch('/se-rating/scsautoservice.asmx',…)` is relative to the page origin → hits mc's :5177 (no proxy) → returns 404 / mc's index.html → SOAP parser sees no PlanRate. Fix: mirrored the EXACT same proxy block to mc's `vite.config.js` `server.proxy['/se-rating']` (target `https://staging.fiadmin.com`, rewrite `/se-rating` → `/scs.webservice`, configure hook strips origin+referer headers). mc's `server.port: 5177` + `strictPort: true` preserved. localStorage flag `blinker.dev.product_admin_mode='proxy'` was already shared (same origin). org_id seeding via `INITIAL_FORM.org_id=102` Apex fallback was already correct. Rule learned: **vite proxies replicate per-host; when an embed runs in a different app's dev server, the proxy MUST be added to that app's vite config too**. Documented in memory. **Wave 22-fu4 (`mission-control d487f59`)** — `fix(copilot): unlock embed persona switcher so super_admin can view API responses inline`. User smoke: embedded AgentView's status-bar header showed STATUS + FORCE STATUS but no PERSONA dropdown — locked behind `personaLocked={true}` on all 3 portal embeds (CoPilotPane lines 279/1085/1250 — protection/refi/insurance). User wanted to switch to super_admin in-place to unlock the "API responses (super only)" link. Original locked decision (PROMPTS.md § co-pilot pane: "personaLocked: true hides AgentView's local switcher" to avoid duplicating mc's top-right global pill) is now SUPERSEDED. Fix: `replace_all` `personaLocked={true}` → `personaLocked={false}` on all 3 sites. mc's top-right pill remains the global default; embed-level switcher is a per-opportunity override. **Auto-mode classifier conservatism re-confirmed** — first dispatch attempt for fu4 was misclassified as a scope-boundary violation despite being a legitimate Agent dispatch (the very pattern the rule prescribes for cross-repo edits). Imperative-only retry succeeded. Memory note already in `feedback_coordinator_dispatch.md`; reinforced. **Wave 22-fu5 (`blinker-platform`, this commit)** — `feat(stoneeagle): cost+org-markup pricing in proxy normalizer`. User smoke: real OMGA UAT GetRates response surfaced raw `<Cost>907.00</Cost>` straight to PlanCard's Total — every markup field on the response (`MarkupMin`/`MarkupMax`/`AdminMarkup`/`BaseMarkup`/`FIMarkup`) was 0 because OMGA configures markup at the platform level, not at SCS. New pure helper `packages/utils/protection-pricing.js#computePlanPrice({cost, retail_rate, regulated_rule_id, bounds}, {orgId, state})` reads `canon/org-registry.json::orgs[].protection_billing.markup` (FL split via `state==='FL'`) and applies per `canon/plan-mappings.json#regulated_rules`: rule 0 (free_markup) → `cost + org_markup`; rule 3 (fixed_retail) → response retail locked, markup ignored; rule 5 (capped_markup) → `cost + clamp(org_markup, MarkupMin, MarkupMax)`; rule 6 (finance_capped) → `cost + min(org_markup, MaxRetailRate - cost)`. `normalizeToFixtureShape` now takes `ctx`, parses `<Cost>` (with `<NetRate>` fallback) + `<MarkupMin>`/`<MarkupMax>`/`<MaxRetailRate>` + `<RegulatedRuleId>`, calls `computePlanPrice`, and emits `base_price = cost + markup_applied` (rounded $) plus the breakdown fields `cost`/`markup_applied`/`markup_regulation`/`retail_rate_response` on each product for ApiResponsesModal transparency. Top-level proxy result also carries `_org_markup: { default_dollars, florida_dollars }` snapshot. `monthly_price` recomputed from new `base_price`. **Fixture mode untouched** — fixture's `base_price` values are already retail-shape (e.g. $2895/$3244/$3692), so `getRatesFixture()` skips the normalizer entirely and continues to render same as before. **Backlog item ADDED**: per-org toggle in admin-console to honor SE response's `MarkupMin`/`MarkupMax`/`AdminMarkup`/`BaseMarkup`/`FIMarkup` fields directly instead of org-config markup (today these are ignored; only `protection_billing.markup` is consulted). Forensic agent (P1-fu3b, `mission-control 230e661`) found two real bugs the prior fixes missed: **(i) actual loop closer** — `updateOpportunity` bumps `updated_at` → `opportunity` ref churns → `handleEmbedVehicleCommitted`'s `useCallback` deps `[opportunity, contacts, ...]` rebuild handler → P1-fu3a's `useCallback`'d EmbedSlot shims (deps `[onEmbedVehicleCommitted]`) rebuild because their input changed → protection AgentView's `onVehicleCommitted` prop ref churns → wizard observer's `useEffect([form.vehicle, onVehicleCommitted])` re-fires. P1-fu3a's "stable shim" claim was empirically false because the chain's root (the handler) wasn't stable. **(ii) why `matched_by:null` always:** `dedupAndUpsertVehicle` had an early-return at `session-data.js:304` that silently dropped when `existing.vehicles` was missing or non-array → match logic never reached → telemetry counted "append" but the write was dropped (explains 19k events / 1 vehicle in UI). **Fix shape (P1-fu3b, +161/-154 in CoPilotPane.jsx, +15/-2 in session-data.js):** ref-based handler — 6 `useRef`s for `[opportunity, contacts, appendVehicleToContact, updateContactVehicle, dedupAndUpsertVehicle, updateOpportunity]`, refreshed in a passive `useEffect` every render. `handleEmbedVehicleCommitted` is `useCallback(..., [])` reading from refs only. Handler ref stable forever → EmbedSlot shims stable → wizard observer's deps don't churn → no re-fire. Helper coerces missing `vehicles[]` to `[]` instead of silent drop, then proceeds through match-or-append. Refs lag by one render but handler is only invoked from embed observers which fire AFTER the parent commit; refs guaranteed current at handler-call-time. **P1-fu3a kept as defense in depth** — shim stability still helps once the handler ref is itself stable. _Earlier (2026-05-07 afternoon → 2026-05-08 morning):_ **Wave 17 P1 + P1-fu + P1-fu2 + P1-fu3 + P1-fu3a LANDED (5 prior commits).** **P1-fu3 (`mission-control 609384e`) fixes a wire-back oscillation surfaced during P1-fu smoke**: cross-sell VIN-collection committed with id `xs_vin_<VIN>` against an existing wizard-committed YMMT-only vehicle id'd `xs_ymmt_<YMMT>`. mc's old dedup at `CoPilotPane.jsx:459-469` (id-OR-both-VIN-OR-both-YMMT) had a critical asymmetric gap: existing-YMMT-only + new-VIN'd-with-same-YMMT short-circuited all three branches → APPENDED a 2nd vehicle to `contact.vehicles`. After append, `opportunity.vehicle_id` ping-ponged between the two ids on each parallel observer fire (protection wizard observer + cross-sell observer), oscillating the left-pane Vehicle card UI. Diagnostic agent identified compound #3 (asymmetric dedup gap) + #4 (unstable callback identity from inline arrow shim at `:700-708` recreating `onVehicleCommitted` ref every render, kicking the wizard observer's deps to re-fire stale YMMT payload). Verification: 0 stale `lib/posthog` imports across mc/protection/insurance/refi; 4 `posthog.js` files deleted; 47 `from 'blinker-platform/telemetry'` import sites; 48 `from 'blinker-platform/components'` import sites; 4 `from 'blinker-platform/utils'` import sites. **All 4 child apps pending `npm install` + browser smoke.** **Wave 15c shipped (smoke pending) + 15b audit shipped + 15a scaffolded.** 9 commits across 5 repos lift `AddressBlock` + `NotesPanel` + `TagPicker` from `refi-portal/src/components/` to `blinker-platform/packages/components/`. Apps will consume via `file:../blinker-platform` and import like `from 'blinker-platform/components'` — `packages/` prefix hidden behind the alias on purpose. **Wave 14 — Admin Console init shipped + Wave 13 follow-ups complete + Wave 14-fu (insurance picker / API responses / mc availableStatuses wiring) shipped.** Closes the only open follow-up gap from earlier this session: insurance-portal AgentView now has a production force-status picker (replaces nothing in DEV — all 5 simulator buttons kept because each has a distinct purpose beyond status forcing) + View API Responses modal (super_admin / `view_api_responses` gate) + `availableStatuses` prop. ## Wave 26 prep — Phase 2 readiness audit (2026-05-10)

After Wave 25 close-out the user paused for a thorough Phase 1 → Phase 2 readiness audit before opening any cloud-infrastructure work. **3 parallel research agents** covered all 6 repos (5 child apps + blinker-platform packages) + 16 memory files + 14 ADRs + 12 canon files + STATUS.md + child-app CLAUDE.mds + the payment-processing-platform reference.

**Headline answer:** Phase 1 prototype is feature-complete and demo-ready. Phase 2 (Google Cloud dev/prod) ready to start AFTER ~2 weeks of focused cleanup (Wave 26 hygiene + Wave 27 hardening) AND a decision session on 5–6 architectural choices that have not been documented anywhere.

**Plan:** `docs/phase-2-readiness.md` (this repo) + `~/.claude/plans/phase-2-readiness.md` (user-local). Plan covers: per-repo Phase 1 state, 3 hidden bugs surfaced by the audit, the `file:` dep model's CI-incompatibility, memory + doc cleanup recommendations (4 archive, 4 update-in-place), backlog prioritization (P0/P1/P2), Phase 2 design decisions (8 numbered with A/B/C options + my recommendations), recommended Wave 26 → Wave 27 → Wave 28+ sequencing. Top-12 items for user discussion compressed into the appendix.

**3 hidden bugs** surfaced by the audit (none flagged anywhere prior):
1. `npm run build` is broken in every portal (lucide-react peerDep fix wasn't reinstalled in child apps).
2. `posthog.init(...)` is NOT called in any portal entry — all 25 waves of `track()` events have been silently dropping to console.
3. `VITE_ALLOW_DEV_VAULT_TOKEN` defaults to `true` in `protection-portal/src/lib/fluidpay.js:63` when the env var is absent → production Vite build without explicit `false` would accept raw card data in the browser → broadens PCI scope by accident.

**Single highest-leverage architectural decision:** the `file:../<repo>` dep model that works perfectly in local dev is structurally incompatible with standard CI/CD. Three options on the table — monorepo conversion (Turborepo/Nx), private npm packages on GCP Artifact Registry, or multi-source Cloud Build. Pick before any CI work starts.

**Status:** Audit complete. Plan promoted into repo at `docs/phase-2-readiness.md`. **Awaiting user-review meeting** on the 5–6 architectural decisions before Wave 26 cleanup dispatch begins.

This wave adds the second SE GetRates call (with VIN), a pure classifier in packages/utils, a new wizard step `RatesChanged.jsx` for the 2 forced-rebranch scenarios, and refund-on-decline wiring through the EFS package shipped in Wave 24 C1.

**Phase B — Packages (parallel-safe — different files):**

**Phase C — protection-portal:**

- New `src/views/customer/RatesChanged.jsx` (Step 9c): 3 CTAs — "Pick a different plan" (via selectPlans on vinRates, with org-config-gated DP-bypass), "Refund and exit" (via refundCharge), "Continue with original" (gated on `allow_over_tolerance_proceed`).
- Wire 9c into `src/views/customer/CustomerView.jsx` step list.
- Handle `form.opportunityFlags.refunded` variant in `src/views/customer/ThankYou.jsx`.
- Add 6 vin-fixture variants under `packages/integrations/product_admin/_fixtures/` for smoke (one per scenario per ADR 17 fixture checklist) + a `blinker.dev.vin_validate_scenario` localStorage key for selection.

**Status: ALL PHASES LANDED LOCALLY.** 5 commits across 2 repos:

- **blinker-platform:**
  - `4ea5400` Phase A — ADR 17 + canon vin_validate config (8-kind classifier contract, per-org config block on all 7 orgs with educated-guess _TODO defaults).
  - `c9b0d23` Phase B1 — `packages/utils/getrates-comparison.js` classifier (175 lines) + 17 passing unit tests + 6 SE-vin fixture variants under product_admin/_fixtures/ (class-changed/ymmt-changed/plan-disappeared/price-lower/within-tolerance/outside-tolerance).

User smoke pending. Push pending. **Wave 25 v3.0.7 closes Task 2 from Blinker Platform v3.0.6 PDF — combined with Wave 24 v3.0.6, all 5 PDF tasks are now feature-complete locally.**

**Refi stage2_result fan-out restoration — wire heavyweight StageTwoResult into wizard** (`refi-portal 6308a1f`, 2026-05-11 morning).
User reported: Agent View terminal screen ("Capture Link Created" → wizard end) used to show refi offers + insurance savings teaser + protection plan teaser stacked in one frame so the agent could pitch the bundle. Today it rendered a slim "Offers returned" card with a literal footnote "Heavyweight Stage 2 fan-out … lifts in a later session." Root cause: not a regression — the § 1.5b lift commit (`refi-portal 3a9ba96`, 2026-05-03) deferred `stage2_result` to § 1.5c, which never landed for this surface. The heavyweight `StageTwoResult` (with `OffersCard`, `QualifiedHandoffCard`, `InsuranceTeaser`, `InsuranceSavingsCard`, `ProtectionPlanTeaser`) was always intact in the monolith (`refi-portal/src/refinance-v2-prototype.jsx:3177-3219`) and already in the monolith's named-export block — just one import away. Fix: (a) new `refi-portal/src/views/customer/StageTwoResult.jsx` re-export wrapper mirroring the `DecisionEngine.jsx` pattern. (b) `RefiWizard.jsx` imports it, adds `selectedOfferId` + `offerConfirmed` local state (parent-owned for OffersCard → confirm → QualifiedHandoffCard transition), and replaces the 52-line slim placeholder block with a `<StageTwoResult … />` render wired to `goBack` / `setStepIdx(0)+reset`. (c) Header comment updated to drop the § 1.5c deferral note. DEV CONTROLS toggles `PROTECTION PLAN SOLD` / `INSURANCE REVIEWED` / `INSURANCE SAVINGS FOUND` were already driving the right form fields — they now visibly drive the fan-out. Vite build passes (330ms). Customer view inherits the same fan-out via the shared `RefiWizard`. **User smoke pending. Push pending.** Dead `refi-portal/src/results/*.jsx` (8 broken function-less extractions, unused, not in build path) noted as optional follow-up cleanup.

**Wave 25-fu2 — classifyRatesChange identity-match by id OR plan_code; DevPanel VIN diagnostic** (`blinker-platform b1a4e9a`, `protection-portal ab75e0f`, 2026-05-10 evening).
User smoke surfaced: plan_disappeared was firing in fixture mode regardless of which plan the user picked — the screenshot showed an identical GOOD plan ($3,800 AAA PT Plus High Mileage) selected at Step 5 AND offered as the first "different plan" option at Step 9c. Root cause: `RecommendedCoverage.jsx` stored `plan.id` (e.g. `"AAA_PT_PLUS_HIGH_48_48000"`) into the `plan_code` field of selectedPlan, but fixture products have `plan_code: null` and the real id in a separate `id` field. `findProductByTriple` in `packages/utils/getrates-comparison.js` compared `"AAA_..."` against `""` → never matched → plan_disappeared every time. The other 4 vin-fixture variants (price-lower, within/outside-tolerance) were silently unreachable for the same reason. The unit-test suite missed it because tests used synthetic plan_codes that matched `product.plan_code` directly. Fix: (a) `findProductByTriple` now takes both `id` and `plan_code`; identity match succeeds when EITHER id-equality OR plan_code-equality holds (id wins when both sides have one). 5 new regression tests pin the fixture-shape (22 total, all green). (b) `RecommendedCoverage.jsx` (2 places) + `RatesChanged.jsx` `confirmRepick` now store BOTH `id: plan.id` AND `plan_code: plan.raw?.plan_code ?? null` on selectedPlan. (c) `VinValidate.jsx` now persists `ratesChangeDetail: detail` alongside `ratesChangeKind` so the diagnostic payload survives the route to RatesChanged. (d) New DevPanel "VIN validate diagnostic" Section in `ProtectionDevControls.jsx` shows selected-plan summary (tier/name/id/plan_code/term/miles/cost/vehicle_class), classifier outcome (kind + per-kind one-line + DevJsonPeek of full detail; for plan_disappeared lists up to 6 available_plans triples so future divergences surface immediately), and vinRates summary. Push pending alongside W25 main + fu1.

**Wave 25-fu1 — RatesChanged refund routes to ThankYou, not eSign** (`protection-portal 3e28804`, 2026-05-10 evening).
User smoke surfaced: clicking "Refund my down payment and exit" on RatesChanged Step 9c was advancing to docuseal/eSign instead of the ThankYou refunded variant. Root cause: `RatesChanged.handleRefund()` correctly called packages `refundCharge()`, set `opportunityFlags.refunded=true`, and called `onNext()` — but `onNext()` is just `setStepIdx(i+1)`, landing on the next step in the static order (`vin_validate → rates_changed → docuseal → thank_you`). Same bug on the manual-review branch (`auto_refund_on_decline=false` orgs). Fix: append a docuseal-skip filter at the end of `buildSteps()` in `protection-portal/src/views/customer/CustomerView.jsx` — when `opportunityFlags.refunded === true || manual_review_requested === true`, drop docuseal from the step list. Added the two new flags to the buildSteps `useMemo` deps. ThankYou.jsx already supported both variants from W25 Phase C — no other changes needed. 11-line diff. Push pending alongside W25 main.

**Open backlog from this wave** (out of v3.0.7 scope; queued):
- Real `/efs-charge/refund` proxy backend (cloud-function endpoint not yet shipped — fixture+emulate are sufficient for sandbox demo).
- Mission-control inbox UX surfacing `opportunityFlags.vehicle_revised` / `rates_changed` / `refunded` chips on agent inbox cards.
- Top-up CTA wiring when `allow_down_payment_bypass=false` and the new plan's min-down exceeds collected DP (currently a stub note in RatesChanged).
- Real per-org tuning of `vin_validate.*` defaults — the seeded values are educated guesses flagged `_TODO`; need partner-ops + finance review.
- Verify SE GetRates returns `vehicle_class` per-product or root-level (classifier currently expects root-level; adjust if per-product).

## Wave 23-fu1 + fu2 — super_admin SE diagnostics + AssetType (2026-05-09→10 night)

**fu1 — super_admin SE-error inline diagnostics** (`blinker-platform ca00b88`, `protection-portal 99c5b4f`).
User triggered an SE error on the wizard (`IMP_CNTR_0068` "VIN could not be decoded") and noted the friendly callout's generic copy hides the diagnostic info super_admin needs. Fix: when `persona === 'super_admin'` and `_error_classified !== null`, the callout adds a Details block (kind, code, internalAction, scrollable raw payload truncated 2000 chars). Agent + consumer personas continue to see only the friendly copy. Persona prop was already plumbed end-to-end. Coordinator side added a "Backlog" section to `architecture/15-se-error-handling.md` for consumer-actionable CTAs (VIN re-enter, state-not-supported "continue without coverage").

**fu2 — AssetType resolution + YMMT data refresh** (`blinker-platform 60a7828`, `protection-portal 427efcd`).
User noticed `<AssetType>T</AssetType>` was hardcoded in the SOAP envelope (line 158 of stoneeagle.js since the file was first written). 2024 Nissan Maxima SR — clearly passenger `P` — was being priced against truck rate filings.
- Root cause: original Wave 17 P1 derivation of `packages/utils/ymmt-data.js` dropped the SEFI CSV's Asset Type column. SEFI uses 3 codes: `P` (passenger), `T` (truck/SUV/van/etc.), `AL` (antique/limited).
- User supplied `stoneeagle_ymmt_reference17APR2026.csv`. New `scripts/regenerate-ymmt-data.cjs` parses it. Decisions: years 2000-2028 (was 2010-2026), Style column dropped (52,457 of 56,137 source rows blank), AssetType stored per-(Make, Model). 71 conflicts within (Make, Model) — mostly subcompact crossovers like Nissan Kicks/Ariya marked both P and T — fall back to first-seen.
- New file shape: 164 makes (was 56), 3,100 models (was 1,230), Maxima trims 9 (was 6). NEW exports: `YMMT_ASSET_TYPE` (Make → Model → P|T|AL), `getAssetTypeForMakeModel()`. Plus new `packages/utils/asset-type.js` (`mapVinAuditTypeToSeAssetType` collapses Sedan/Coupe/Wagon/etc → `P`, everything else → `T`; default `P`).
- `stoneeagle.js` reads `input.asset_type` from caller, validates P/T/AL, defaults to `P` when missing/invalid. Hardcoded `T` gone.
- protection-portal consumer: VehicleAdd captures asset_type from VIN decode (`mapVinAuditTypeToSeAssetType(vinAuditResult.type)`) AND YMMT lookup (`getAssetTypeForMakeModel(make, model)`); submit-time derivation persists to `form.vehicle.asset_type`; GarageLocation + RecommendedCoverage plumb into `getRates`. **Local `src/constants/ymmt-data.js` + `src/lib/vinDecode.js` deleted** (consolidated onto packages — closes part of the Wave 17 P2 backlog for protection-portal; refi-portal still has local copies, queued).
- User Q on revert: their CSV was actually MORE complete than the prior repo file (164 vs 56 makes). 2024 Maxima isn't missing — Nissan discontinued the model after 2023. Decision: keep the regen.

**Term-semantics finding pulled into backlog** (`blinker-platform <pending commit>` for ADR 14 + STATUS).
User asked "how did you determine this?" pointing at PlanCard rendering "Omega EXCL Total Miles" as `+72 mo / +100,000 mi`. Honest answer: the `term_semantics.by_regulated_rule_id` defaults in canon `plan-mappings.json` were educated guesses, every entry flagged `_TODO: confirm with SE doc`. Real signal is per-plan-code, not RegulatedRuleId — and at least three semantics exist (additive / absolute_from_contract / absolute_from_purchase) where my schema only modeled two. Backlog in `architecture/14-term-semantics.md`: authoritative mapping derived from (1) auditing real SE GetRates response fields for term-basis signal, (2) enumerating unique (TpaCode, ProductTypeCode, PlanCode) triples from real proxy traffic, (3) for each unique plan code, reading the actual contract document, (4) building plan-code-keyed canon mapping. **No name-pattern guessing.**

## Wave 21 — StoneEagle eRating package lift (2026-05-09 after midnight)

### Recurring lucide-react resolution issue

**Status:** Peer-deps fix landed in `blinker-platform/package.json 9ce41ed` (Wave 19) — declares `peerDependencies: { react, react-dom, lucide-react }` so the platform package signals "host provides these." Child apps (mc + protection-portal) already carry `lucide-react ^1.8.0`. **However**, mc + protection-portal haven't run `npm install` since the peer-deps fix landed, so their `node_modules/blinker-platform` symlink still resolves through the old peer-deps-less platform `package.json`. Build still trips during `npm run build`; **dev mode (`npm run dev`) works because of host-app hoist**.

**User smoke action:** `cd mission-control && npm install && npm run build` and `cd protection-portal && npm install && npm run build` to refresh file: dep resolution. Wave 20 verification depends on this.

### Coordinator dispatch lessons (W20)

- **Sequential dispatch on shared-storage races**: v3-C and v3-B both touch `src/lib/super-admin-storage.js`. Sequenced explicitly to avoid race; v3-B's brief acknowledged v3-C's just-landed shape and extended without conflict.
- **Research → build pattern**: v3-A's read-only research output (1 markdown file) was the spec input for v3-B. Cleaner than embedding research instructions in the build agent's brief — the research doc is durable and re-readable.
- **Cross-repo agent (BL)** demonstrated pragmatic shape-compat decision: Option (b) `coerceOption` shim in RelationshipPicker accepted both shapes, avoiding cascading edits in refi's downstream readers. The research-then-decide pattern at the agent level worked cleanly.

- Cross-repo Task 1 demonstrated coordinator-pattern for canon + packages + child-app wiring in a single agent brief — agent dispatched changes across 3 repos without coordination friction.

## Wave 16 — Smoke-fix dispatch (2026-05-05)

**Trigger:** User ran post-Wave-15c-fu smoke test of `Mission Control → Add Contact → Start Opportunity → {Refi | Insurance | Protection}` on 2026-05-05. Feedback surfaced 8 distinct issues over 4 rounds; planning session resolved them into 9 numbered fixes (F1–F9). User decisions captured in plan: F5 mock-now-abstract-next; F2 vehicle-gating-per-workflow; F8 image-7-shape (cross-sell Confirm-contact form is the new canonical insurance lead-capture UX).

**Dispatch shape:** 1 platform commit (coordinator-touch) + 4 parallel sub-agents (mission-control, protection-portal, insurance-portal, refi-portal). Cross-repo prop contracts spec'd in plan: `requireVin` (refi VehicleAdd), `showOriginationForm` (insurance AgentView), `locationBias` (AddressBlock).

No behavior change for existing callers.

Was unconditionally advancing to `'contact'` and re-rendering the picker.

Wired into `handlePickType`, `handlePickContact`, `handleContactSaved` for the zero-vehicle branch. Removed insurance-only block in `handleSkipVehicle`. Flipped `canSkip` to `true`. Insurance added to SkipFooter wizard label. Mount `useEffect` for the seeded zero-vehicle case (defensive for future callers passing seededType). Existing telemetry preserved (`vehicle_skipped: true` + `auto: true` for the new automatic path).
- `c5a4673` `fix(StartOpportunityFlow): hydrate picked from seed props on open (Wave 16 F1-fu)` — user smoke 2026-05-06 reported F1 still broken: AgentHome → "+ Add contact" → save → Refi was still landing on the contact picker (with the new contact at the bottom of the list). Root cause: `StartOpportunityFlow` is mounted permanently in AgentHome's tree (returns null while `!open`), so `useState(initialPicked)` ran ONCE at AgentHome mount when `seededContact` was still null — `picked.contact` was stuck at `null` permanently, so F1's `if (picked.contact)` short-circuit never fired in the seeded path. Fix: new `[open]` effect hydrates `picked` + `step` + ancillary state from current seed props each time `open` flips to true. F1's handler logic was correct; the bug was the prop→state sync.

Stripped the DOB input + form-state field + `dobInputRef` + `Calendar` icon import. Canon contact record still carries `date_of_birth: null` at create time; insurance-portal `LeadOriginationForm` populates it. **Note:** an earlier conservative interpretation kept MC's StartOpportunityFlow `'dob'` step alive; user reversed that on a later smoke pass — see `78710de` below.

Removed: `'dob'` step taxonomy entry, `pendingDob` / `pendingVehicle` state, `handleDobSubmit`, `DobStep` component, DOB-gate JSX branch, `dob_gate` telemetry events, back-handler `'dob'` case. `patchContact` prop dropped from `StartOpportunityFlow` and from `AgentHome`'s call site (sole consumer was `handleDobSubmit`). `runVehicleSkipFlow` now unconditionally finalizes the opp regardless of `contact.date_of_birth` presence. Local `lastFiredVehicleKeyRef` dedupe removed — mc-side idempotent patch path owns dedupe. Canonical `market_value` slot confirmed: `form.vehicle.market_value` (`{retail, trade_in, fetched_at, source}`) populated by protection's data-write effect at `VehicleDrive.jsx:82-101` (kept post Wave-16-F3 visual-card removal).
- *(mc)* `4bbf193` `feat(CoPilotPane): receive onVehicleCommitted from protection wizard, append + bind to opp (Wave 16 F2-fu11a)` + *(protection-portal)* `f99e186` `feat(AgentView): fire onVehicleCommitted when wizard Step 1 commits (Wave 16 F2-fu11b)` — user smoke 2026-05-06: vehicle entered in protection wizard Step 1 (VehicleAdd) didn't propagate to mc's session contacts, so CoPilot left "Vehicle" card stayed at "No vehicle yet" for the entire wizard. Cross-repo prop contract: protection's `AgentView` accepts optional `onVehicleCommitted({ id, year, make, model, trim, vin?, source })` callback; fired via a `useRef`-deduped `useEffect` observer on `form.vehicle` (NOT step-transition coupled, so the VIN/YMMT mismatch-modal `chooseMismatchResolution('vin')` branch and `persistVehicleAndAdvance(...)` both flow through cleanly). Deterministic ids — `xs_vin_<VIN>` or `xs_ymmt_<Y_M_M_T>` — collapse re-mounts + back/forward through Step 1 to a single mc-side append. mc CoPilotPane handler dedupes against existing `contact.vehicles` (id/VIN/YMMT) before `appendVehicleToContact + updateOpportunity({ vehicle_id })`. Inbox-row vehicle label rebuilt on bind. Standalone protection callers (App.jsx) leave the prop undefined and the observer early-returns — backwards compat. **Phase 2 note:** `xs_vin_*` / `xs_ymmt_*` id scheme is a Phase 1 stand-in; Phase 2 should mint vehicle ids server-side via `blinkerApi.contacts.appendVehicle(...)` and reconcile via the returned record. Three callers now format the same vehicle label (`buildNewOpp`, `OpportunityContextPane`, this new handler) — meets 3-strikes rule for a `formatVehicleLabel(v)` util in mc/utils or `packages/utils`.
- *(protection-portal)* `9b792ce` `feat(CaptureLinkForm): drop "Note (optional)" textarea — NotesPanel owns notes (Wave 16 F2-fu9)` — completes the parallel triple (insurance `fe65def` + refi `3db0c6f` + protection `9b792ce`). The textarea + `contact.note` form-state field gone; `partnerData.contact.note` and `has_note` telemetry rerouted to source from `opportunity?.notes`. **Surfaced gap:** protection's `AgentView.jsx:312` wires NotesPanel to a LOCAL `useState('')` per a Wave-12a Phase 1 decision ("ephemeral session state, no persistence; mission-control owns the persisted activity log"). Sibling repos (refi `opportunity.notes`, insurance `workflow.notes`) persist into the form/workflow shape and the right-rail notes ride along to the EI payload. Protection's right-rail notes today do NOT ride along — `opportunity?.notes` is always undefined. Net: the EI capture-link payload's `note` field is now always `null` (was the inline-textarea content before). Either (a) accept ephemeral right-rail behavior in protection per Wave-12a intent, OR (b) lift the `useState('')` to read/write `form.notes` (or similar persistent slot) so right-rail content rides along. Pending user direction.
- *(refi-portal)* `e123a16` `feat(ScreenVehicleAdd): inject VinAudit-returned values into YMMT pickers when fixture lacks them (Wave 16 F2-fu8)` — user feedback 2026-05-06: VIN `1GTH5BEA8J1149222` (Chevy Silverado, trim "Work Truck") decoded fine but YMMT_DATA fixture didn't carry "Work Truck" → user got `Trim "Work Truck" not in list — please select.` error + an empty Trim picker (no valid option to pick) → Continue gated. Fix: when VinAudit returns a make / model / trim NOT present in `YMMT_DATA`, inject the value into `form.extraMakes` / `extraModels` / `extraTrims` (new `INITIAL_VEHICLE_FORM` fields) AND auto-select it. `YmmtPicker` surfaces extras alongside the YMMT_DATA base list (deduped). User-driven hierarchy changes (manual pick of a different make / model) clear the now-stale downstream extras. VIN clear/reset clears all extras. The trim-mismatch error branch in the decode useEffect is gone — extras absorb the case end-to-end. F2-fu7's `trimMismatchError` predicate + `(!requireVin || trimMismatchError)` Trim picker exception retired (the case it guarded against is now structurally unreachable). Single source — applies to refi standalone + mc StartOpportunityFlow + insurance-portal AgentView pre-step automatically.
- *(other repos canon-sync)* `mc 55e6366` + `protection a078afa` + `customer 5b04279` — `canon: sync 2026-05-06-org-timezone`. Pulls the `timezone` field bump into each child app's `src/constants/canon/`.
- `4cd1436` `feat(LeadStatusTimeline): TZ-aware times + day separators + per-event detail (Wave 16 F2-fu5)` — three bundled asks against the agent's LEAD STATUS panel. (1) **Per-event detail** rendered under each past step from the webhook payloads (`workflow.capture.verification` / `workflow.quote.payload` / `workflow.policy.payload`). Capture step shows detected carrier + policy # + vehicle + "View ID card" link when `source=id-card`. Quote step shows quoted carrier + premium + savings (annualized hint, null state for `quote_only`). Quote-Viewed shows `viewedAt`. Policy-Bound shows carrier + `id` (with a TODO note that EI's `summary.policy` is thin per architecture/06 open question #4). Off-path quote-phase error surfaces the captured carrier when verification ran first. (2) **Timezone-aware times** — `Intl.DateTimeFormat` with the org's TZ (e.g. `1:48 PM`); reads from `canon/org-registry.json` via `orgId`, falls back to browser-local TZ on miss. (3) **Day separators** — horizontal divider with date in the middle (`May 6, 2026`) above the first past event of each day, and between past events whose day-in-org-tz differs. AgentView passes `workflow.orgId` through to LeadStatusTimeline (both lean post-send and full-agent post-send mounts). `fmtTime` helper retired.
- **F2 deviation:** brief asked for `VehicleAdd` lazy-imported from `refi-portal/src/views/customer` with `requireVin={true}`, but insurance-portal **does not declare refi-portal as a dep** (intentional per Wave 15c — commit `dd6e115` "drop refi-portal" from package-lock). Re-adding the dep just for this surface is out of scope. Substituted a lightweight inline VIN+YMMT block that satisfies the `Generate-link disabled until 17-char VIN` rule without the cross-repo import. Future wave: lift to `packages/components/VehicleVinBlock`.

**refi-portal (Agent 4, 2 commits):**
- `b263e11` `fix(VehicleDrive): restore valuation fetch + dev mock fallback (Wave 16 F9)` — Maria's canonical fixture VIN `4T1BF1FK0FU987654` added to `MARKETCHECK_VIN_CACHE` ($12,450 / $23,070); FNV-1a-seeded deterministic mock as final dev-only fallback after cache miss. Gated on `import.meta.env.DEV` so prod still surfaces real outages.

When `true`: `ok = hasVin && hasManual && !form.vinDecodeError`. Inline italic hint renders below OR divider when VIN empty. Refi standalone callers byte-identical.
- **YMMT-only valuation card extension skipped** as low-cost-only bonus — non-trivial because the debounced `useEffect` at line 2157 has the exact early-return we'd need to extend AND the cache/mock fallback chain currently keys off VIN. Filed as **F9-fu** for next wave. Agent 2's F3 deferral confirms F9-fu is on the critical path.

### Cross-repo prop contracts established

- **`requireVin`** (boolean, default `false`) — refi-portal `ScreenVehicleAdd`. Used by MC `StartOpportunityFlow`'s "+ Add new vehicle" path AND insurance-portal LeadOriginationForm's inline vehicle block (when wired in a future wave) for refi/insurance VIN-required gating. Refi standalone callers byte-identical.
- **`showOriginationForm`** (boolean, default `true`) — insurance-portal `AgentView`. Used by protection-portal `CrossSellSubFlow` to suppress the embedded LeadOriginationForm so cross-sell renders only its own Confirm-contact form (F6).
- **`locationBias`** (object, optional) — `packages/components/AddressBlock`. Forwarded verbatim to Google Places "New" REST autocomplete body. ZIP also auto-appended to the input string regardless of locationBias.

### Wave 16 deferred items

1. ~~**F3 + F9-fu**~~ — **Shipped 2026-05-06**:
   - `refi-portal 2a92679` `feat(ScreenVehicleDrive): YMMT-only valuation fallback (Wave 16 F9-fu)` — relaxed early-return so YMMT-only entries trigger valuation; new `fetchMarketCheckPriceYmmt` skips the real API (it requires VIN) and goes straight to the deterministic FNV-1a dev-mock with a YMMT-derived seed (`year|make|model|trim`). Refi's inherited card is now the only visual valuation surface; protection's data-write keeps the canonical shape live for downstream readers.
   - **Optional bonus still deferred:** lift refi's MarketCheck card to `packages/components/VehicleValueCard` for cross-app consumption (Wave 13b's "shared component" intent). Tracked alongside the broader `packages/components/VehicleAddOrConfirm` lift.

All 4 agents halt-and-reported correctly on cross-cutting surprises (F3 YMMT regression, F7 destination ambiguity, F2 dep-direction conflict in insurance-portal, YMMT bonus non-triviality in refi-portal).

''` (handles 0), `type` prop (default `'text'`), `optional` flag (renders `(optional)` suffix). `SelectField` adopts mc's `appearance-none` + ChevronDown chrome + keeps protection's string-or-object options branch (refi's `EMPLOYMENT_TYPES` is `string[]`); empty option goes from `disabled` (mc) to selectable `Select…` (refi/protection). `PhoneField`/`DateField` lifted verbatim from protection. `TextAreaField` lifted verbatim from mc.

**Phase B — telemetry expansion (1 commit in blinker-platform):**
- `42ca2c9` `feat(packages/telemetry): expand surface with registerEvents + getEventCatalog` — `_registry = new Map()` module-scope; `registerEvents(events)` accepts `[{ name, props?, description? }]` arrays (idempotent — re-registering same name overwrites); `getEventCatalog()` returns array; dev-mode unknown-event warn (`_isDev()` checks `import.meta.env.DEV` first, falls back to `typeof window !== 'undefined' && !window.posthog`); registry empty → no warning (consumer hasn't opted in yet). `captureEvent(event, props)` retained as `@deprecated` alias for insurance-portal's pre-15c-fu API name.

**Phase C — validators superset (1 commit in blinker-platform):**
- `d746231` `feat(packages/utils): lift validators superset` — 240-line `packages/utils/validators.js` with TWO coexisting families. **string|null family** (refi/protection idiom; returns the error message OR null): `validators.{required,email,usPhone,ssn,zip,state2,vin,flexDate,flexDateInPast,positiveCurrency,positiveInt}` + `parseFlexDate(v)` returns `{ month, day, year, date } | null` + `parseDob(v)` returns `Date | null` + `ageYears(v)` integer + `dobAdult(v)` composes flexDateInPast + 18 ≤ age ≤ 110 + `formatPhoneDisplay(s)` for `(###) ###-####` + `sanitizeNumeric(v, opts)`. **boolean family** (mc idiom): `isValidEmail`, `isValidUSPhone10`, `normalizePhoneE164`, `normalizeZip5`, `isValidZip5`. mc's workflow-specific helpers (`HOUSEHOLD_RELATIONSHIP_KINDS`, `findContactMatch`, `buildHouseholdRelationship`, `validateContactForm`) deliberately NOT lifted — they're contact-form-modal-specific dedupe logic, not platform validators.

**Phase D — child-repo sweeps (8 commits across 4 repos, sequential):**
- `mission-control` (2 commits, `8f21011`+`8d746d9`): rewires 18 files to `blinker-platform/components` + `blinker-platform/telemetry` + `blinker-platform/utils`; deletes 4 files (JsonPeek + WizardShell + FormFields + lib/posthog.js — mc kept its local DevPanel.jsx). mc's `lib/contact-form.js` re-exports the lifted boolean validators from blinker-platform/utils for back-compat.
- `protection-portal` (2 commits, `764eb6c`+`68b8522`): rewires 20 files; deletes 6 files (JsonPeek + WizardShell + DevPanel + FormFields + lib/posthog.js + lib/validators.js).
- `insurance-portal` (2 commits, `1412cf3`+`2391b51`): rewires 11 files; deletes 5 files (chrome quartet + lib/posthog.js).
- `refi-portal` (2 commits, `a61eca3`+`4055678`): rewires 10 files; deletes 6 files (chrome quartet + lib/posthog.js + utils/validation.js).

**Cross-repo verification:**
- `grep -rn "from '.*lib/posthog'"` across mc/protection/insurance/refi: **0 lines** ✓
- 4 `posthog.js` files: **gone** ✓
- `from 'blinker-platform/telemetry'`: **47 import sites** ✓
- `from 'blinker-platform/components'`: **48 import sites** (chrome quartet + pre-existing trio) ✓
- `from 'blinker-platform/utils'`: **4 import sites** (validators consumers) ✓

**Discoveries during 15c-fu (filed as deviations from brief):**
1. WizardShell and FormFields in mc were entirely dead code (not imported anywhere in mc/src) — agent deleted them with no consumer impact. The audit's "byte-identical" framing was true for the 3 active consumers; mc's "near-identical" file just happened to be unused.
2. JsonPeek's mc variant differed in chrome (`border-t` vs `rounded-md`, no `?? {}` fallback) — mc was rewired (per brief: only DevPanel stays local) — mc's only consumer is App.jsx and the visual delta is a minor card-chrome upgrade.
3. refi-portal monolith (`refinance-v2-prototype.jsx:598-747`) carries duplicate validator copies that re-export to PrequalForm. Out of scope this wave; PrequalForm continues importing from the monolith re-export. Track for future cleanup.
4. The audit's broken `getSequence` stub in `refi-portal/src/utils/validation.js` was already gone (Wave 15c deleted it). The remaining file content was clean and lifted whole.
5. **Insurance snake_case events flagged but NOT renamed**: `insurance_lead_origination_started`/`_failed`/`_duplicate`, `insurance_capture_card_uploaded`/`_submitted`, `insurance_consumer_link_created`/`_sent`, `insurance_webhook_received`/`_quote_viewed_simulated`/`_policy_switch_clicked`. They ride forward via `captureEvent` (the `@deprecated` alias). Future surgical pass should migrate to dotted form (`insurance.agent.*`/`insurance.customer.*`).
6. Stale comment references to `src/shared/DevPanel.jsx` exist in 4 dev-controls files (informational only, not functional). Left untouched.

**Smoke gate (now applies to BOTH 15c trio AND 15c-fu chrome quartet):** user runs `npm install` per app + dev-server smokes mc/protection/insurance/refi. Same Vite-cache fallback applies (`rm -rf node_modules/.vite` if anything looks stale).

**Memory anchor:** `~/.claude/projects/-Users-.../memory/project_platform_packages.md` captures the Wave 15 arc + dep direction rules so future sessions starting in any sibling repo can quickly orient. `MEMORY.md` index updated.

**Dep direction rules (load-bearing for all future waves):**
1. `packages/*` MAY read `../canon/*.json`.
2. `packages/*` MAY import sibling `packages/*`.
3. `packages/*` MUST NOT import from any child app.
4. Child apps consume via `file:../blinker-platform` only — never deep paths.
5. Each `packages/<name>/index.js` is the only allowed import target.

**NeverBounce + Twilio Lookup decisions locked (for Wave 15d):**
- Provider: NeverBounce only at launch; abstraction layer supports kickbox/zerobounce/mailgun drop-in via canon enum.
- Validation policy: advisory (legacy default; failed verification still saves with warning). Per-org override via `org-registry.json::contact_validation.email.policy = 'advisory' | 'blocking'`.
- Permission: super_admin only configures globally OR per org.
- Twilio Lookup: extends existing `twilio` integration with `lookup_enabled` flag (no separate provider — same creds).

**Verified:** packages tree (15 files); `node --check` passes for all 6 `index.js` files; root `package.json` `exports` map valid (`node -e "require(...)"` parses cleanly); git log shows 3 commits with correct identity (`dealercrm <chad@carcarepeople.com>`); working tree clean.

**Wave 15b — read-only audit, COMPLETE.** Single 562-line report at `docs/wave-15b-audit-report.md`. Top findings: (a) Wave 15c is unblocked — the trio has 6 confirmed cross-app consumer sites all going through the public surface, ZERO deep-import violations against the trio itself; (b) highest-value next category is `telemetry/` — 4 near-identical PostHog shims + 161 distinct event names + confirmed snake_case-vs-dotted drift inside insurance-portal; (c) `shared/` chrome quartet (`DevPanel`, `JsonPeek`, `WizardShell`, `FormFields`) is duplicated 3-4× and meets 3-strikes today (JsonPeek + WizardShell byte-identical between protection/insurance/refi; mc's DevPanel has additive divergence — `JsonBlock` + collapsible Section + different prop shape — lift the smaller shape first, mc converges in a follow-up); (d) two pre-existing dead-code issues — 7 broken file fragments in `refi-portal/src/components/{DevPanel,EmbeddedEntry,FormFields,JsonPeek,Stage2Shell,TopBar,WizardShell}.jsx` (header imports + orphan function bodies, NOT exported, NOT imported anywhere) plus `refi-portal/src/utils/validation.js:180` `getSequence` declared with empty body — both compile because nothing imports them; both should be deleted as part of 15c-hygiene; (e) two anti-pattern deep-imports (`mission-control/src/components/CoPilotPane.jsx:18` reaches into protection-portal's CustomerView; same file L122 lazy-imports refi-portal's RefiWizard) + one hardcoded `VINAUDIT_API_KEY` duplicated 3× across refi-portal + protection-portal — none block 15c, fold into 15d integrations work. **Ranked top-10 extraction backlog** at the bottom of the report:
