## Phase 1 build order

1. ✅ **`protection-portal/`** — substrate up; customer view 11/11 + agent view shipped; `AgentView` exported as public component for mission-control. Phase 1 acceptance complete pending real-browser smoke (VinAudit CORS + FluidPay sandbox creds).
2. 🟡 **`mission-control/`** — AgentInbox + CoPilotPane + ContactProfile done; canon-conformant fixtures (contacts ✅; notes + activities ✅ on top-level shape after `promote-note-activity` bump — payload sub-shape field-name drift remains for the Phase 2 producer to reconcile); **next:** notes/activity persistence story (session-only today — pick Phase 2 `blinkerApi.notes.create` vs Phase 1 localStorage shim)
3. ✅ **`insurance-portal/`** — full EI-real-contract rework + canon cleanup pass + SavingsCard public export, **now consumed in production by protection-portal Confirm** via `file:../insurance-portal` dep. Phase 1 complete pending real-browser smoke + PostHog real client.
4. 🟡 **Phase 1.5 — cross-sell orchestration** — protection-portal becomes orchestrator; refi joins as second embed source. Sub-items:
   - 4a. ✅ **Docs + canon** (`2026-05-03-cross-sell-orchestration`) — ADR `architecture/08-cross-sell-orchestration.md`, contract `architecture/02-integration-boundaries.md`, canon `org-registry.json` cross_sell block, README decisions, PROMPTS.md §§ 1.5b–1.5d.
   - 4b. ✅ **`refi-portal/` substrate + customer view** — three commits in refi-portal: `6f47eee` (flatten refi-prototype/ to root), `f68deb6` (substrate scaffolding — port 5179, DEV CONTROLS, ViewSwitcher, shared primitives lifted from protection-portal), `3a9ba96` (customer view + RefiWizard composition + 13 screen wrappers via re-export from `src/refinance-v2-prototype.jsx` monolith). Plus platform-side commit `59b945d` adding refi-portal to `scripts/sync-canon-into-apps.sh`. Build clean (335 KB / 100 KB gz, 1753 modules); dev server smoke 200 at :5179. Public surface intentionally empty pending § 1.5c. **Notable solve:** the prototype's `src/screens/*.jsx` files were broken stubs (every one missing its function declaration line — beyond the "halt if 10+" threshold). Agent worked around by re-exporting intact screens from the prototype's monolith `src/refinance-v2-prototype.jsx`, saving ~3000 lines of duplicate code. **Screen-sequence drift:** PROMPTS.md sketched a sequence by filename; actual prototype `getSequence(form, hasCoApp)` differs (auto_loan + credit BEFORE applicant cluster; co-app cluster reorders by credit band). RefiWizard follows source. Worth re-validating in § 1.5c against `documentation/refinance-version-2-wiki.md`.
   - 4c. ✅ **`refi-portal/` agent view + hybrid mode + public exports + DEV CONTROLS port-forward** — five commits in refi-portal across two agent sessions (the first hit a rate limit after Chunks A+B): `fb02102` (Chunk A — `runDecision()` + `useRefiPrequal()` extracted to `src/lib/refi.js` as pure function), `f31da6e` (Chunk B — 14-section DEV CONTROLS port from monolith DevPanel), `1089157` (Chunk C — AgentView shell: CaptureLinkForm + AgentChrome + SaveAndSendFooter + NotesPanel + ApiResponsesModal + `src/lib/posthog.js`), `65eb902` (Chunk D — public exports: PrequalForm new slim form, OffersCard + QualifiedCard lifted from prototype EmbeddedPost; thorough JSDoc embed contract on `src/views/customer/index.js`), `73a9de0` (Chunk E — lift form/dev state to App; wire DEV CONTROLS to wizard for prefill apply, jump-to-screen, force-result, force-partner, reset). Build clean (386.37 kB / 114.86 kB gz, 1767 modules). **Browser smoke deferred to user** — Chunk E agent verified module transforms + production build but didn't exercise the UI in a real browser; the spec's listed checks (prefill apply, jump-to-screen, force-result disqualified, force-partner Gravity, reset) are wired per code paths but unverified end-to-end. Pre-existing structural break in `src/constants/mock-data.js` (four missing array/object terminators) was found + fixed inline in Chunk C (the breakage only surfaced once a second consumer imported MOCK_OFFERS). PostHog events emitted: `refi.agent.{capture_link_generation_started,capture_link_created,capture_link_sent,save_and_send,note_added,persona_switched,status_overridden,api_responses_viewed}` + `refi.prequal.submitted`. **Public surface confirmed** per `architecture/02-integration-boundaries.md`: PrequalForm + OffersCard + QualifiedCard from `src/views/customer/index.js`; AgentView from `src/views/agent/index.js`; `useRefiPrequal()` + `runDecision()` from `src/lib/refi.js`. **Refi-portal NOT pushed to GitHub** per user directive ("hold refi pushes until 1.5+").
   - 4d. ✅ **`protection-portal/` embed wiring** — five commits in protection-portal: `45ce983` (file:../refi-portal dep + HMR header), `b72a99d` (`src/lib/protection-pricing.js` with PMT + ELIR + `protectionPlanMonthlyOnRefi` legacy formula port + `insuranceMonthlySavings` + `effectiveMonthly` — separate functions, no model conflation), `a1822de` (RecommendedCoverage CTAs + per-org gating + buying-power UI on plan cards), `c15944b` (consumer sub-flow with breadcrumb back to coverage; embeds insurance mini-capture + refi PrequalForm with full result-branching against all 4 result types), `e6a1fd3` (agent side-pane composition + DEV CONTROLS toggles + force-complete actions). Math worked example verified: `protectionPlanMonthlyOnRefi({ planTotal: 3692, loanPrincipal: 3692, apr: 0.0899, termMonths: 60 }) → $76.63/mo` (within tolerance of expected ~$77). Bundle 374.39 → 508.88 kB raw / 111.28 → 147.10 kB gz. **Chunk 4 grew bundle +116.72 kB raw** from refi-portal monolith pull-in via `useRefiPrequal` (the prototype's full decision engine + validators + DISQUAL_REASONS + ZIP_FALLBACK ride along). React + lucide-react properly deduped. Code-split candidate for § 1.5e. PostHog: 12 `protection.cross_sell.*` events. **Apex 102 enables both CTAs; all other orgs disable both** with "Not enabled for this org" tooltip. Browser smoke deferred — user should hand-verify both consumer sub-flow (URL changes to `?substep=…`, breadcrumb visible, back returns) and agent side pane.

   **In-flight at session end (uncommitted):** `refi-portal/src/constants/index.js` is structurally broken upstream (7 missing array/object terminators from § 1.5c Chunk B's constants lift; refi-portal's own build doesn't trip it because the app entry doesn't import the barrel — only protection-portal's embed surface hits it). 3 of 7 closing brackets added in refi-portal's working tree (CREDIT_BANDS, OWNERSHIP_OPTIONS, EMPLOYMENT_TYPES); 4 remaining (RELATIONSHIP_OPTIONS, DISQUAL_REASONS, DEFAULT_ORG_CONFIG, ZIP_FALLBACK). Coordinator paused awaiting user authorization to complete the fix vs revert (the protection-portal vite-plugin shim auto-deactivates if upstream is fixed). § 1.5d's `45ce983`–`e6a1fd3` are committed and don't depend on the fix landing.

   **Spec gaps surfaced by 1.5d for § 1.5e to address:**
   1. **Refi-portal `src/constants/index.js` half-fix** — finish the 4 remaining closers OR revert the 3 applied (see "In-flight" above).

Today protection-portal synthesizes a placeholder offer for the `offers_returned` branch using `protectionPlanMonthlyOnRefi()` with org-default APR/term.

   6. **`eslint.config.js` missing** in protection-portal (pre-existing).

   **Spec gaps from 1.5c (carried forward):** (a) `PrequalForm.onComplete(decision)` returns `{ result: 'pre_approved' | 'offers_returned' | 'disqualified' | 'pending', ... }` — protection-portal embed needs to branch on `result` to render OffersCard / QualifiedCard / disqualified-card / pending-card. **Refi-portal exports OffersCard + QualifiedCard but NOT a DisqualifiedCard or PendingCard** — protection-portal either renders its own or pings refi-portal to add to the public surface; recommend adding before § 1.5d for symmetry. Documented inline in AgentView; not blocking § 1.5d but flag for the eventual mission-control refresh. (f) `useRefiPrequal({ contactId?, vehicleId? })` returns `{ prequalState, decision, offers, submitPrequal, reset }` — protection-portal can either consume `PrequalForm` directly or call `useRefiPrequal` directly + custom UI. Both paths public. (g) Browser smoke not done in Chunk E session — user should hand-verify DEV CONTROLS toggles before § 1.5d depends on the wired state.

- ~~**`note` and `activity` canon blocks are still _TODO stubs.**~~ ~~**Activity types not enumerated in canon.**~~ ✅ **Resolved 2026-05-03 (`promote-note-activity` bump):** note + activity canon blocks landed; mission-control fixtures conform on top-level shape. `_payload_shapes` block in canon `activity` documents per-`type` payload contracts; mission-control's fixture has field-name drift inside payload (e.g., `action` vs canon's `action_name`, `from`/`to` vs `from_status`/`to_status`, `partner`/`event` vs `partner_name`/`event_name`) — validator doesn't flag (payload is opaque) and producer-side fix lands when the Phase 2 activity API ships.
- **Tag color/category convention.** Custom-color tags use `tag.color` as a hex; the rendering applies it as a tinted background + border. Canon `tag.color` is documented as "mirrors GHL's per-tag color convention" — confirm GHL stores hex (or named color, or a small palette) when the syncer lands. Category prefix proposal in canon's tag block is still _TODO and tag-clicked is a no-op stub today.
- **`opportunities.json` still uses denormalized `contact_name` + `vehicle` strings.** Once canon `opportunity` block lands and references `contact_id` + `vehicle_id` only, drop those fields from the fixture and resolve via lookup. Today's denormalization is a perf optimization that's also a source-of-truth ambiguity (if a contact's name changes, opportunities go stale).
- **`payments` workflow** still has no `ghl-status.json` block — AgentInbox falls through to regex bucketing.
- **Persona routing exclusivity** (super_admin → SuperHome unmounts AgentInbox) and **HMR caveat with file: deps** carry over from the previous session.

### `insurance-portal/` ✅ PHASE 1 COMPLETE (PENDING REAL-BROWSER SMOKE + REAL POSTHOG CLIENT)

**Owner:** active session
**Status:** substrate :5176 + customer capture wizard shipped on the two-phase Embedded Insurance contract. `?view=customer` → CaptureForm collects carrier / expiration / vehicle confirm / optional card upload → `submitCapture(payload, { nextOutcome })` returns `{ leadId, status: 'received', receivedAt }` in ~60ms → `updateWorkflow({ lead, status: LEAD_CREATED })` → wizard advances to GettingQuote → wizard subscribes via `subscribeWebhooks(leadId, onEvent)` → ~1500ms later mock auto-fires `status.verification.completed` (or `status.error` / `status.duplicate` depending on `nextOutcome`) → wizard resolves `evt.machineId` to STATUS via a 3-line literal table and updates `workflow.capture` + `workflow.status`. `GettingQuote` renders distinct copy per terminal status (CAPTURE_COMPLETED / ERROR / DUPLICATE). `submitCapture` schedules webhooks internally and `simulateWebhook(leadId, eventName, opts)` cancels any pending auto-fire so manual override always wins; multi-subscriber per leadId so agent view + customer wizard can both listen. `src/fixtures/embedded-insurance-webhook.json` checked in (GEICO / $1,842/6mo / 50/100/50 BI/PD / $1k/$500 deductibles / expires 2026-09-14). `status-map.js` collapsed to 35 lines: `STATUS` generated from `canon.insurance.statuses` via `Object.fromEntries(... [machine_id])`, plus `getInsuranceStatus(machineId)` and `machineIdForExternalEvent(eventName)` helpers — canon is the source of truth, no hand-maintained map. DEV CONTROLS: "Next webhook will be" Segmented (Completed / Error / Duplicate) wired to `dev.nextWebhookOutcome`, JsonPeek shows both `workflow` and `dev` slices. Three new PostHog events stubbed: `insurance_capture_submitted` → `insurance_capture_received` → `insurance_capture_webhook_received`.
**Note:** decisions resolved this session — (1) `received` will NOT become a canon status (it's partner wire-protocol, not a Blinker domain concept; `lead.created` covers the post-POST state from our perspective); (2) `MACHINE_ID_TO_STATUS` literal in `CaptureForm` stays — explicit beats clever for a 3-entry table; (3) agent view will use `subscribeWebhooks` directly, not polling — webhook is the live path, polling lands later as a separate `getLeadStatus(leadId)` recovery endpoint if/when needed; (4) OCR'd shape promoted to canon as `_insurance_extracted_shape` block in `ghl-fields.json` so agent view + quote review + SavingsCard all read the same definition; (5) "Started" status `crm_stage: null` documented in canon — agent view bucketing code must handle null.

CaptureLinkForm has email/phone validation, mock contact prefill (Jordan / 2021 Toyota RAV4), Generate button (synthesizes URL `?view=customer&token=CAPTURE_<24-hex>`, locks once a link exists; status flows started → lead.created → capture_link.created), Send button (logs `[twilio:mock]` + `[mandrill:mock]` payloads, fires `insurance_capture_link_sent`, locks with timestamp). LeadStatusTimeline reads `canon.insurance.statuses` directly via `getInsuranceStatus(machineId)`; subset list (MAIN_PATH_MACHINE_IDS, TERMINAL_OFF_PATH_MACHINE_IDS) validated against canon at module-load time so a re-sync that removes a referenced status throws on import. Status-transition timestamps tracked in AgentView local state (kept agent-specific, not on workflow shape). Phone stored 10-digit, prepended `+1` at submit.

**Note:** decisions resolved this session — (1) `capture_link.viewed` lives as `signal_source: blinker_internal` (URL-shortener telemetry), not partner — promoted to canon explicitly; (2) `signal_source` discriminator added to every insurance status (`partner` | `blinker_internal` | `agent_action` | `consumer_action` | `system`) — makes implicit nullness explicit; (3) `ins_capture_link_url` + `ins_capture_link_token` + `ins_quote_link_url` + `ins_quote_link_token` promoted to canon `insurance_specific` — these round-trip through GHL contact records; (4) Generate button locks once link exists (re-running needs Reset workflow) — chose explicit gate over regenerate-with-new-token, because regen needs an audit story (old token invalidated? both sent?) the prototype shouldn't blur; (5) MOCK_CONTACT lift to shared lib deferred — two copies in two apps acceptable for prototype. **Open issue (small):** `submitCapture`'s 1500ms auto-fire timer starts at Generate, not Send, so the timeline can race past `capture_link.sent` if the agent waits. ### Data layer — ✅ source-of-truth declared, validation in place

- `architecture/07-data-layer.md` declares **Blinker DB as source of truth** for the platform's domain entities (contact, household, opportunity, vehicle, note, activity, tag). GHL is reframed as one of several downstream sync targets (alongside PostHog, EI, StoneEagle, FluidPay/EFS, DocuSeal, VinAudit). Documents the API-layer mocking pattern: Phase 1 = per-app `src/fixtures/*.json` conforming to canon shapes; Phase 2 = `blinkerApi.{entity}.get|list|search` returning the same shapes; the swap is intentionally a one-line import change in each consumer. Captures Phase 2 entity write rules and the tags-vs-opportunities working answer.
- **No app currently uses an api-mock pattern.** Per-app fixtures remain the Phase 1 vehicle. Lift to `blinker-platform/api-mock/{entity}.json` happens when a second app needs the same entity (today only mission-control reads contacts/opportunities); both apps would then import via the existing `file:` dep convention (same as `protection-portal: "file:../protection-portal"` for AgentView).
- `scripts/validate-fixtures.js` (Node, stdlib only) walks each child app's `src/fixtures/*.json`, matches filenames to canon entities, and warns on shape drift. Lightweight by design — JSON parse + top-level field comparison only, no type/format enforcement (Zod/JSON Schema upgrade lands later). Exit 0 when no errors; warnings allowed. Partner-payload fixtures (`embedded-insurance-*.json`, `stone-eagle-*.json`) correctly skip as not Blinker-domain.
- **Validator nuance — over-warns on canon-optional fields (surfaced 2026-05-03-promote-note-activity sync).** Validator treats every key in a canon `shape` block as expected-on-every-record; it does not parse the trailing `?` optionality marker on canon value strings (e.g., `"opportunity_id": "string? — FK..."`). When any record in a fixture omits an optional field, the union-of-missing surfaces in the per-file warning. Hit on mission-control's `notes.json` (`edited_at`, `opportunity_id`) and `activities.json` (`actor_id`, `opportunity_id`) — fixtures are correct; validator is being conservative. Two fixes available: (a) teach the validator to skip "missing" warnings when canon value-string starts with a known type token followed by `?`; or (b) fold into the Phase-2 schema upgrade (Zod/JSON Schema) where required-vs-optional is first-class. (a) is ~30 minutes; (b) is right but not soon. Default: defer to (b) unless the noise gets in the way of catching real drift.

- ✅ `architecture/07-data-layer.md` — written 2026-05-03; Blinker DB as source of truth, sync targets inventoried, Phase 1 → Phase 2 mocking pattern documented

### Sync script — ✅ ready

`scripts/sync-canon-into-apps.sh` — executable, copies canon into each child app's `src/constants/canon/`. Run after any canon change.

## Decision log

Locked decisions are in `README.md`. Add new locked decisions here with date + brief rationale.

| Date | Decision | Rationale |
|---|---|---|
| 2026-05-02 | Polyrepo + meta-repo (this repo) | AI context budget; independent deployability; partner access; small shared concerns |
| 2026-05-02 | Vite + React 19 + JS (no TS) + lucide-react substrate | Match refi prototype + payment-processing-platform; no learning curve |
| 2026-05-02 | DB source of truth, PostHog augments | Reconciliation safer; PostHog wins on drift |
| 2026-05-02 | 1 config per org (Blinker → Parent → Child); copy-down (no inheritance) | Avoid the legacy's 4 configs per AAA pattern (RCO/BUSA/PA/NIC); keep simple |
| 2026-05-02 | VIN OR manual YMMT, mismatch = confirmation step (never silent) | Legacy production bug: silent Bronco → Bronco Sport substitution |
| 2026-05-02 | Org IDs are canonical across all systems | Confirmed via legacy MC + EFS prototype + GHL Apex cross-ref (Apex=102) |
| 2026-05-02 | `customer-portal/` is the public/partner-embed front door; imports from canonical `*-portal/` apps | Separates public-tuned chrome from canonical workflow apps |
| 2026-05-02 | Phase 1 build order: protection-portal → mission-control → insurance-portal → customer-portal | Customer self-serve PDF mockup is most spec'd; mission-control composes ports; customer-portal imports ports |

## Risks & open issues

- **Naming inconsistency:** `payment-processing-platform/` doesn't follow `*-portal/` convention. Acknowledged; not worth the rename lift. (`Refinance Application Version 2/` resolved 2026-05-03 — renamed to `refi-portal/`.) Local↔GitHub asymmetry on refi: local dir is `refi-portal/`, GitHub remote is `refinance-prototype` — accepted; the GitHub UI rename can happen later as a non-destructive follow-up.
- **AAA Prospect A is paused at orchestrator level (EFS) but active at MC level:** test record `User#25325` got through MC but its payment plan wouldn't have been actively serviced. Worth confirming as expected behavior or as a config drift.
- **Refi status mapping has many `** TBD **` rows in the spreadsheet:** will block canon completeness for refi flows. Plan: have product clarify before refi work picks up.
- **PostHog project key:** not yet provisioned for the prototype phase. Each child app's `.env.example` should reference `VITE_POSTHOG_KEY` but starter sessions can stub.
- **Consumer Self Serve PDF mockup missing on disk:** `protection-portal/docs/mockups/` is empty. Customer-view UX is currently informed by legacy walkthrough screenshots + refi prototype patterns. If the canonical PDF exists, drop it in before VehicleUse onward so copy/layout matches.
- **VIN-decode-vs-manual-YMMT confirmation step (locked decision):** ✅ implemented in protection-portal VehicleAdd as of 2026-05-02 — side-by-side modal, both flow directions (VIN-first, manual-first), resolution recorded on `form.vehicle.source`. **Pending UI smoke verification** because the implementation calls VinAudit's live API through `fetchVinDecode()` and Cowork sandbox CORS may block it. Manual-first path resilient regardless. Verify in a real browser before checking acceptance.
- **VinAudit live API in sandbox:** protection-portal VehicleAdd's VIN decode hits VinAudit live, not a fixture. If sandbox CORS blocks the request the mismatch modal won't fire (manual-first works fine). Two future options: (a) capture a fixture and route through it for sandbox, (b) wire a small proxy. Not urgent yet — nothing else depends on it being mock'd.
- **Legacy walkthrough coverage gap (VehicleUse + Modifications):** screens 06-/07- in `BlinkerLegacy/.../current-state/screens/` are payment-options screens, not vehicle use / modifications — earlier coordinator prompt framing was off. Refi prototype also lacks these screens. So VehicleUse + Modifications were built from CLAUDE.md/README spec only, with no legacy walkthrough or refi reference. If product clarifies canonical UX later, those two screens will need a copy/layout pass.
- **Refi-prototype paste artifacts:** ongoing pattern — sessions have found truncated function signatures on shared components (mission-control, insurance-portal) and triple-declared `YMMT_DATA`/`YMMT_MAKES` constants in `ymmt-data.js` (protection-portal). Anyone lifting from refi-prototype source should expect to clean up similar artifacts (relevant for any remaining refi lifts in customer-portal).
- **FluidPay sandbox creds unwired:** protection-portal `BillingPayment` uses dev-fallback tokenization by default (no `VITE_FLUIDPAY_PUB_KEY` / `VITE_FLUIDPAY_TOKENIZER_URL` set). Live tokenization path is lifted from EFS with both load-bearing workarounds intact (`setTimeout(0)` defer + Guardian-bypass `submit()`) but **not exercised against real sandbox credentials in this session**. SANDBOX_TEST_CARDS list available in `lib/fluidpay.js` for when creds get wired. Phase 1 acceptance for billing should not flip to ✅ until a real-browser smoke test against sandbox credentials passes.
- **Customize "re-run selector" UX assumption:** protection-portal `Customize.jsx` re-runs `selectPlans()` with the user's adjusted criteria (term/miles/deductible) and keeps them in their original tier where possible. This wasn't in any spec — built from CLAUDE.md mention only. Alternative UX: present a list of in-tier alternatives matching their adjustments instead of re-running the selector. **Worth a product confirmation pass** before customer view ships externally.
- **DocuSeal collapses two legacy agreements into one:** legacy (screens/12-) signs Product Agreement + Payment Plan Agreement in sequence. Phase 1 protection-portal `DocuSeal.jsx` is a single-page placeholder. Second-agreement screen lands when DocuSeal goes live in Phase 2 — flagging so the canon expansion (per-agreement template IDs, ordering rules) gets scoped at that time, not now.
- **Cross-sell premium absent from SavingsCard savings variant:** when `savingsAmountCents > 0`, SavingsCard renders the annualized savings headline + per-period savings line, but the absolute partner premium (e.g. Geico $1,200/6mo) is only shown in the no-savings variant. Protection-portal Confirm cross-sell context might want the premium visible alongside the savings figure; the lever is `copyOverrides.subtitle` on the existing public surface, or a small SavingsCard tweak to surface premium in the savings variant. Phase 2 product call.
- **Cross-sell partner-config gate:** protection-portal Confirm uses a DEV CONTROLS toggle as the Phase 1 proxy for whether the cross-sell renders. Phase 2 needs a per-org config flag (similar pattern to FluidPay routing per org). No code path yet — when org-config canon lands (already tracked in pending canon work for `down_payment_percent`), this is one more flag to model.
- **Real-EI tail-fetch on Confirm:** the cross-sell currently uses a fixture quote (`src/fixtures/insurance-cross-sell.json`). Real EI integration would require contact's vehicle + applicant data through `createLead` + `getLeadLink` + a wait for `quote.completed` — adds latency at a sensitive moment in the protection flow. Phase 2 product call: fetch real quote, show spinner, or stay fixture/preview-only?
- **HMR caveat now applies in two places:** mission-control's `file:../protection-portal` dep already had the "restart dev server after upstream changes" caveat. Protection-portal's new `file:../insurance-portal` dep inherits the same. Documented in each consumer's header comment. Not blocking, just dev-loop friction.
- **PROMPTS.md cross-sell prop-shape doc drift:** the cross-sell prompt referenced `copyOverrides: { title?, cta? }` but SavingsCard's actual JSDoc + source uses `{ title?, subtitle?, ctaLabel? }`. Implementation followed the source, not the prompt. Minor doc churn — fix the prompt template the next time it's updated.
- **Protection-portal becomes the highest-coupling app (Phase 1.5).** Three `file:` deps will land: `insurance-portal` (already wired), `refi-portal` (planned § 1.5d), plus mission-control's reverse dep on protection-portal's AgentView. Build size grows; HMR surface widens to three upstream sources. Track bundle size before/after each embed lands. Integration testing is harder — a break in `refi-portal/src/views/customer/index.js` only surfaces when protection-portal builds, not when refi-portal builds. Manual smoke after any embed-source change until CI lands.
- **HMR caveat propagates to a third dep.** Mission-control's `file:../protection-portal` dep already required dev-server restarts after upstream changes; protection-portal's `file:../insurance-portal` inherited it; § 1.5d's `file:../refi-portal` makes it three. Document the caveat in `RecommendedCoverage.jsx` header once the embed wires in.
- ~~**`refi-portal` rename + GitHub publish is a manual user step.**~~ ✅ **Resolved 2026-05-03.** Local rename done (`Refinance Application Version 2/` → `refi-portal/`). GitHub remote already published at `github.com/BlinkerGit/refinance-prototype`. App source remains nested at `refi-portal/refi-prototype/`; § 1.5b will flatten as part of substrate. § 1.5b is unblocked.
- **Math-model conflation risk (Phase 1.5).** Insurance is savings framing; refi is affordability framing. Code, copy, and analytics MUST keep them separate (two PostHog events, two `form.*` keys, two functions in `protection-pricing.js`). Do not introduce a unified `crossSellResult` shape — see `architecture/08-cross-sell-orchestration.md` § Risks for the rationale.

## How to update this file

When a child app finishes a chunk:
1. Open the relevant `### app/ section` above
2. Check off the acceptance items
3. Update **Status:** line (⏳ → 🟡 → ✅)
4. If the completion unblocks downstream work, update the downstream app's **Blockers:** line
5. Bump the **Last updated:** at top
6. Commit this file's change in `blinker-platform/` repo
